請參考末頁之免責宣言說明1

投資評等  強力買進

士電(1503)  
目標價位  250 元

獲利成長至 2028 年無虞

公司治理評鑑排序： 
1 2 3 4 5 6 7

個股基本資料：

徐培勛 
鋼鐵、重電產業 
S1120023@tss-c.com.tw 
 股價評價基礎： 
AI、半導體帶動用電需求激增，士電已接獲客戶需求，重電 T4 廠產能將於
4Q27 量產 ，2H28 開始貢獻營收 ；2027~2028 進入表後儲能工程認列高峰 ，
預估 2026/2027/2028 稅後 淨利 YoY+29%/+33%/+15% ， 稅 後EPS 
8.10/10.80/12.46 元，給予強力買進評等，目標價 250 元(20x 2028 EPS)，
潛在漲幅 22%。

營運近況摘要： 
士電成立於 1955 年，為台灣老牌變壓器生產商，技術來自三菱重工的技轉，
1H26 產品組合：電力配電(變壓器、配電盤)72%、車輛零件 15%、自動化設
備(工具機)10%、其他 3%。內外銷比 65%：35%。客戶包含：Mitsubishi 
Electric/Mitsuba Electric/ Panasonic/ Hitachi ABB/ Siemens/泓德能源/起
而行/和泰車。目前三個廠區都在新竹：電裝廠、新豐廠及重電廠。

2026 年重電 T1+T2 廠產能利用率提升，T3 廠、表後儲能小幅貢獻 
預估 2026 年營收 415 億元(YoY+12%)，因變壓器需求殷切，重電 T1、T2
廠產能利用率提升至 90%，重電 T3 廠 2025/9 開始量產，Lead time 1~1.5
年，4Q26 開始認列營收，2027 年全面貢獻；且公司在手 500MW 表後儲能
(太陽能儲能)統包案也開始貢獻營收 ； 毛利率21%(YoY+0.2ppts)， 因產品組
合較同期相同； 業外則有 金融資產 公允價值、匯兌利益挹注，稅後淨利
YoY+29%，稅後 EPS 8.15 元。

2027~2028 年 T3、T4 廠產能提升，表後儲能進入認列高峰 
展望 2027~2028 年，重電內銷(佔重電 80%)：政府希望強韌電網計畫完工
期限從 2033 年提早至 2028 年，依 2027 年預算書依然維持 2033 年完工，
2026/2027/2028 資本支出 46/118/155 億元，維持成長態勢。重電外銷(佔
重電 20%)： 為了因應全球 AI、半導體等強勁用電需求，重電 T4 廠將於
2027/9 量產，預期2H28 開始貢獻營收，2029 年全面貢獻，T1+T2 年產能
10GVA(十億伏安)，T3 可貢獻產能 5GVA、T4 再貢獻 5GVA，T4 主要客戶
為日本、美國電力市場，並專注在 345KV 變壓器生產。國內表後儲能(太陽
能)：公司在手 500MW 表後儲能案，若假設 2026/2027/2028 完工比率
10%/40%/50%計算，預計貢獻 7.5/30/37.5 億元，

說明： 
一、 公司治理評鑑成績高者顯示其公司治理較符合主管機關要求，適合 1 年以上之長期投資或存股標的，與投資評等(強力買進等)僅 1
年內之投資建議不同。 
二、 依臺灣證券交易所及證券櫃檯買賣中心委託證券暨期貨市場發展基金會辦理的公司治理評鑑，每年將受評公司區分為 「上市公司」 、
「上櫃公司」 兩組，由70 項指標(1.維護股東權益及平等對待股東 11 項，占 16%，2.強化董事會結構與運作 18 項，占 25%，3.提
升資訊透明度 7 項，占 10%，4.推動永續發展 34 項，占 49%)編製而成，各組依評鑑成績高低分別公布七級距之公司名單，台新
投顧係引用民國 114(2025)年度之評鑑結果資料。 
三、 轉登證基會警語：本評鑑系統使用之評鑑方法受有限制，評鑑工作善盡相當之注意義務以確認各評鑑指標之執行及資訊透明，惟評
鑑結果僅顯示該企業之公司治理在本評鑑指標下之運作狀況，不宜過度仰賴，使用者必須自行衡量資訊的價值，主辦單位及執行單
位不為使用者之資訊決策及結果負擔任何責任。

## Document 013: 20260910-AAPL新品發表會

### Page 1

Apple
秋季新品發表會
2026.09

### Page 2

iPhone 18 Pro與iPhone 18 Pro Max推出
• 蘋果新一代高階旗艦智慧型手機 ，在智慧功能、處理效能、電池續航與攝影系統四方面迎來
重大突破。外觀及材質採用同色調配色的鋁金屬邊框結合一體成型背面玻璃 ，提供4種配色，
包括太空黑、銀色、冰川藍以及勃根地紅。
• 螢幕互動介面：動態島縮小，支援同時顯示多達 3 個即時動態。
• Siri AI整合：由最新的Apple Foundation Models與端側模型驅動，具備擬真人聲、自訂語
速抑揚頓挫、螢幕感知、跨App執行指令與全系統精確聽寫。
• iPhone接力通話：允許兩部 iPhone共享同一個電話號碼 ，拿起任一部手機即能無縫通話
（首波與T-Mobile、德國電信合作，後續納入Verizon與EE）。
• AirDrop速度提升達 80%，行動網路與 Wi-Fi 自動無縫切換。
1
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 3

18 pro系列核心處理器升級：A20 Pro 晶片
• 先進製程：採用最新2nm製程技術打造，具備更高的電晶體密度。
• 6核心CPU：配置2顆桌面級全新超級核心，效能提升達20%，為智慧型手機中最快核心；搭配4顆
高能效核心，並全數整合神經加速器以驅動低延遲裝置端AI。
• 7核心GPU：圖形運算效能較前代大幅躍升 40%，微架構針對3A級手遊優化；配備全新神經加速
器，其8位元浮點運算吞吐量提升達2倍，可於本機直接運行第三方大型語言模型。
• 雙神經網路引擎：額外配置第二組神經網路引擎，核心總數達32核心，本機AI運算能力倍增。
• 記憶體介面：記憶體頻寬大幅提升50%，為 iPhone 歷來最寬的記憶體匯流排介面。
• WMCM封裝：借鑒Apple M系列晶片架構，採用全新特製封裝，將晶圓裸晶與記憶體並排佈局，
移出SoC主要熱路徑，使晶粒直接與均熱板貼合，提升散熱效能。
2
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 4

散熱系統與續航快充
• 新一代散熱Vapor Chamber：表面積為iPhone 17 Pro的3倍，內部去離子水持續循環導熱；
均熱板採用80%回收不銹鋼製成，並搭配高導熱石墨片。
• 奈米雙晶銅材料：業界首創散熱材料 ，能增強機身結構剛性，並快速將熱量自顯示螢幕引導
排除。
• 持續效能釋放：高負載下持續效能較iPhone 17 Pro提升 40%，為iPhone 16 Pro及更早機
型的2倍。
• 電池續航力：(1)iPhone 18 Pro：機身小巧，但擁有與iPhone 17 Pro Max相同的全天候續
航；純 eSIM 機型之影片播放時間達 36 小時；真實綜合日常使用模型下可達 24小時。(2)
iPhone 18 Pro Max純eSIM機型影片播放時間高達 45 小時；綜合使用模型下每次充電可達
30 小時。有線快速充電：充電約 15 分鐘可充至 50% 電量；充電 5 分鐘可提供約 6 小時影
片播放時間。
3
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 5

專業相機系統
• 4,800萬像素Fusion主相機：搭載可變光圈 ，採用6片雷射切割葉片 ，葉片厚度薄於人類頭髮 ，由特製聚
合物複合材料製成；搭配微型轉子機構與客製光學鏡片組。
• 可調節光圈：暗光環境自動切換至 F1.8 大光圈，進光量增加約 50%，提升夜間人像與星空攝影品質；光
線充足時縮小光圈以加深景深，確保群體合照邊角與前後背景清晰。
• Pro Controls：提供相機手動操作介面，支援手動微調4檔光圈、白平衡、直方圖曝光監控、手動快門速
度調整。
• 錄影與對焦升級：支援拍攝後添加電影級景深且開放60 FPS半速慢動作；支援4K Dolby Vision HDR縮時
攝影；Audio Mix升級聲音與音樂演算法；智慧對焦追蹤利用AI本機模型牢牢鎖定進出畫面的動態主體。
• 攝影風格升級：新增照片紋理與膠卷顆粒感調節，可即時調整膚質質感並支援無損復原。
• 影像真實性：除相容SynthID標記AI生成圖外，推出Apple Reference Image憑證機制。感光元件在拍攝

瞬間即在像素層級對照片進行安全密碼學簽署 ，私有雲端運算會沖印成一份不可修改的數位負片 ，與一
般可編輯相片共同儲存於照片App中，以證明照片原始未經變造。
4
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理

### Page 6

蘋果首款摺疊旗艦：iPhone Duo
• 蘋果推出首款旗艦摺疊機iPhone Duo，將iPad級別的大螢幕整合至小巧機身中，採書本式對折，非對稱
式對角圓角線條，展開時為iPhone歷來最薄機身，核心組件高度整合在相機凸起處內部 ，留出內部空間
配置雙電池模組。螢幕正面為Ceramic Shield 2、背面為Ceramic Shield，並具備完整IP68等級防塵防水。
• 機身：邊框由5級鈦金屬精工切削並呈現拋光鏡面質感；天線斷點處導入 陶瓷纖維增強嵌件強度為5級鈦
金屬的3倍。
• 內外螢幕：展開為7.6 吋Super Retina XDR螢幕，具窄邊框與一致的長寬比，外螢幕：5.4 吋顯示螢幕，
具備相當於iPhone 18 Pro 90%的可用顯示面積。內螢幕影片可播放31小時，外螢幕播放達44小時，雙
螢幕平均綜合使用續航達24小時。
• 後置鏡頭：4,800萬像素 Fusion 主鏡頭(支援2x光學品質望遠、4K 120 FPS慢動作錄影與4K杜比視界縮
時)+4,800 萬像素 Fusion超廣角鏡頭。

• Apple Pencil支援：雙螢幕皆支援Apple Pencil(USB-C)，為iPhone首度支援Apple Pencil手寫與繪圖。
• 側邊Touch ID：於側邊電源鍵整合TouchID指紋辨識(可註冊多組指紋)，亦支援Apple Watch自動解鎖。

6
AAPL
資料來源： AAPL、中信投顧整理
• A20 Pro晶片：採用2奈米與裸晶直貼散熱封裝 ，配合大型均熱板，持續效能較 iPhone 17
Pro提升35%；內建全新顯示引擎 ，可同時驅動雙螢幕並消除螢幕下鏡頭邊緣像素過渡走樣 。
• Apple C2晶片：深度整合基頻、RF收發器、PMIC與通訊韌體，上傳速率較上一代C1X提升
達50%，功耗降低15%。在美國市場正式加入5G mmWave頻段支援。

### Page 8

iPhone Duo無摺痕螢幕技術
• 鉸鏈：由超過100枚精密微型零組件與碳纖維承載支撐板組成 ，可變扭力曲線讓開闔阻尼手
感順暢，並整合磁鐵陣列牢固吸合。
• 螢幕防護多層貼合結構：表面採用奈米紋理抗眩光表面 ，利用無光罩雷射微影技術逐像素雕
刻微透鏡，呈現視覺平整霧面感，使中央摺痕在視覺上近乎隱形 。表層覆蓋高抗刮客製聚合
物，剛性提升40%。OLED面板上下皆夾有高強度超薄玻璃 ，並灌注黏彈性膠材 ，摺疊時各
層能如書頁滑移，避免反覆摺疊疲勞形變；底層襯有鈦金屬板強化支撐。
• 雷射製造公差控制：組裝以AI演算法將鉸鏈與外殼進行公差吻合配對；出廠前以共軛焦雷射
逐台掃描表面拓撲，並3D列印沉積多達25層光固化聚合物微層，消除微波紋。

### Page 12

相關台股供應鏈
• 全新(2455)、穩懋(3105)：因Wi-Fi 7相較於Wi-Fi 6在手機中新增6GHz的頻段，同時由於對PA性能要求，
將轉向使用GaAs作為材料。我們認為相關供應鏈如全新(2455)、穩懋(3105)，將受惠於手機廠導入Wi-Fi 7
，下半年PA相關台股供應鏈營運展望正向，若後續iPhone新機銷售表現佳，有望進一步對獲利有所挹注。
• 大立光(3008)：大立光受惠iPhone 18新機拉貨動能，且今年新旗艦機首度搭載可變光圈鏡頭，並且為主要
供應商，為2H26營運增加動能。大立光切入CPO應用，有利於推升評價，雖短期營收能見度有限，但預估
將於2028年實現量產並貢獻營收，有望成為自手機鏡頭外公司第二成長動能。過往大立光在手機鏡頭本業
的營運上升週期下 ，預估本益比約交易在 15至18倍左右，而公司成功切入CPO元件市場，市場現給予25-
30倍以上本益比左右。
• 奇鋐(3017)：過去iPhone 17 Pro 首度導入超薄 VC，隨AI 負載提升，我們預期 VC 將逐步成為蘋果高階手

機、平板及折疊旗艦標配。折疊機預計採用雙片式超薄VC，帶動單機用量與ASP倍增；我們預期奇鋐可取
得約50%市占率，有望明顯受惠於蘋果高階手持裝置散熱需求成長。
• 新日興(3376)：在折疊機軸承(Hinge)方面，Apple為實現極致的無感摺痕與耐用度 ，軸承內部結構更為複
雜，零件數及加工精度均較現有非蘋產品大幅提升，帶動單機軸承模組 ASP 接近倍增。台系供應鏈中，我
們認為新日興(3376)受惠程度最高。新日興與主要組裝代工廠鴻海(2317) 透過合資關係合作，有助於整合
機構件製造與組裝，並提升初期量產良率；我們預期其有望取得 Apple 首款折疊機的高階軸承訂單，成為
新日興中長期獲利的重要成長動能。
• 鴻海(2317): 鴻海負責蘋果首款摺疊機的整機組裝與最終調整，並與軸承廠新日興深化合作，共同搶攻高精
密轉軸商機。高毛利的新機效應將顯著拉抬整體產品組合與獲利表現。結合 AI 伺服器業務的持續高成長，
未來營運展望樂觀。
11資料來源：中信投顧整理

HDI concerns easing
New capacity and price hikes boosting earnings visibility for 2027-28
We are increasingly confident in Gold Circuit’s transition towards higher-layer-
count HDI boards, supported by meaningful investments in technology, talent, and
new capacity. The planned capacity ramp aligns well with the next-generation

ASIC schedules of two major CSP customers, while potential layer-count increase
from 26 layers for Trainium3 to 34 layers for Trainium4 should drive meaningful
content growth. Meanwhile, PCB price hikes and improving cost pass-through
should support a sequential GM recovery from 3Q26. We raise our 2027/28 EPS
forecasts by 1%/9% and lift our TP from NT$1,955 to NT$2,000 while reiterating
our High Conviction Outperform rating.
Less concerned about the HDI transition

While the market remains concerned about Gold Circuit’s HDI capabilities, we
believe the company has recruited experienced talent and invested meaningfully in
HDI technology to support future ASIC demand. The second Thailand plant and
new capacity at the first Suzhou plant are scheduled to contribute from 2H27,
followed by the second Suzhou plant in 1H28, with all three expansions primarily
dedicated to HDI production. Importantly, the capacity ramp should align well with

the next-generation ASIC schedules of two major CSP customers. We expect Gold
Circuit to maintain its leading position in AWS Trainium4 and benefit from
meaningful content growth, driven by an increase in PCB layer count from 26 layers
for Trainium3 to 34 layers for Trainium4.
Price hikes underway
For AI accelerator PCBs, we believe Gold Circuit should gradually pass rising CCL
costs through to customers. Our checks suggest that non-AI PCB products are

being quoted at 3-4x prior price levels, with lead-times extending to at least four
months at leading PCB vendors. This indicates a tightening industry supply-demand
environment and suggests that the GM weakness seen in 2Q26 should not persist
in the coming quarters. We forecast GM to improve sequentially to 36.7%/36.8%
in 3Q26/4Q26.
Earnings revisions and valuation
We keep 2026SP EPS largely unchanged but raise our 2027/28 forecasts by 1%/9%
to reflect higher production value contributed by the new plants and increasing HDI

content. Accordingly, we raise our TP from NT$1,955 to NT$2,000, still based on
30x 2H27-1H28SP PE, and reiterate our High Conviction Outperform rating.

### Page 5

HDI concerns easing Gold Circuit - HC O-PF
14 September 2026 5
Investment thesis
We expect Gold Circuit to benefit from the strong CCL/PCB material upgrade trend,
rising shipments of AI servers and continued spec upgrades in CPU servers, which
require higher PCB layer counts. The upgrade in networking towards 800G should
also lead to improving margins for the company.
Catalysts
1) Accelerating server shipments driven by US CSPs and enterprise customers; 2)

server content value growth; 3) a stronger-than-expected networking demand
recovery; 4) a notebook demand recovery.
Valuation details
We value all of our coverage in the PCB/CCL space on PE as it better reflects
margins and earnings outlooks, which we believe the market will mainly focus on.
We use a target PE multiple of 30x, in line with the downstream server sector
average after the recent rerating driven by AI. Given GCE's higher-than-peer
revenue exposure to cloud servers and switches, its leading market position, and

our expectation of robust AI/ASIC server shipments with computing server demand
to gradually recover, we think 30x PE is justified as server/PC ODM suppliers have
all rerated to the 15-30x range on the back of the booming AI demand. And as GCE
also generates a similar number of sales from server and AI, it will likely enjoy strong
growth (better content growth from server and switch) and better returns (less
competition) than assemblers in the mid-to-long term.

Investment risks
Downside risks: (1) order adjustment as a result of chip shortages or supply chain
disruption; (2) weaker-than-expected cloud/datacentre Capex demand; (3) slower
adoption or delayed launch of Intel’s Eagle Stream and AMD’s Genoa platforms; (4)
further raw material price hikes, and (5) more severe competition across the
industry.

## Document 061: 2344華邦電 群益

Source PDF: all report pdf/2344華邦電 群益.pdf

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 17 日

1 群益投顧 
華邦電 (2344 TT) 宣 布 將 以 全 現 金 11.2 億美元 方 式 取 得 Infineon (IFX  DE )的 NOR Flash
與 F-RAM 事業 部 100% 股權 ， 本 交 易 預 計 於 2H27 完成 。 此 次 收 購 對 華 邦 電 而 言 ， 可
藉此取得 較 完整 NOR Flash 線 ， 並 新 增 F-RAM 產品與相關全球客戶、研發資源，進
一步提高在 NOR Flash 市 占 率。目 前 記 憶 體 報 價 上 漲 有 利華邦電 短 期 獲 利 增 加，矽電
容 則為新 獲 利 來 源 ， 中 長 期 則 可 望 受 惠Spansion 收 購 效 益 ， 因 此 對 華 邦 電 建 議Buy 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw  
華 邦 電 宣 布 將 以 全 現 金 方 式 取 得 Infineon 的 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM 事業
100% 的股權 ：  
09/16 /2026 華 邦 電 發 布 重 訊，宣 布 將與 德 國 半 導 體 大 廠 Infineon (Infineon )旗
下的 Infineon Technologies LLC 簽 署 股 份 購 買 協 議 。華 邦 電 將 以 全 現 金 約
11.2 億 美 元 ( 以 匯 率 31.6 估 算 約 新 台 幣 353.92 億元 ) ， 取 得 Infineon 
Technologies LLC 旗下 NOR Flash 與 F-RAM 事 業 經 重 組 後 標 的 公 司 的 100%
股權。  
交 易 完 成 後 ， 華 邦 電 將 直 接 或 間 接 持 有 標 的 公 司100% 股 權 ， 標 的 公 司 並 將
重 新 以 「Spansion 」 為 名 營 運 ， 形 成Winbond 與 Spansion 雙 品 牌 布 局 。Spansion
總 部 續 留 美 國 加 州 聖 荷 西(San Jose )，保 留 原 Infineon /Cypress 的 研 發 核 心 與
歐 美 技 術 團 隊(跨 十 餘 國 人 才)。  
交 易 總 金 額 將 依 Stock Purchase Agreement 所 約 定 之 交 割 日 財 務 狀 況 及 價 格
調 整 機 制 結 算 後 確 定，資 金 來 源 為 自 有 資 金 或 融 資 活 動 支 應。本 交 易 預 計 於
2H27 完 成 ， 惟 仍 須 取 得 相 關 主 管 機 關 核 准 ， 並 達 成 或 依 法 豁 免 股 份 購 買 協
議 所 約 定 之 其 他 交 割 先 決 條 件 。 
華 邦 電 透 過 此 交 易擴充 高階 NOR Flash 和 新 增 F-RAM 產 品 線 ， 市 占 穩 居
NOR Flash 第一 ：  
此次收購 對 華 邦 電 而 言 ， 則 可 藉 此 取 得 完 整 NOR Flash 線 ， 並 新 增 F-RAM
產 品 與 相 關 全 球 客 戶、 研 發 資 源，進 一 步 提 高在 NOR Flash 市 占 率 。依 Omdia
統 計 ，2025 年 全 球 NOR Flash 市 場 規 模 約 34 億 美 元 ， 其 中 華 邦 電 市 占23%
居 首 ， 其 次 為 兆 易 創 新(603986 SHA) 及旺宏 (2337 TT) ， 各 占 17% ， Infineon
占 11% 、 排 名 第 四 。 
從 產 品 及 客 戶 結 構 來 看，雙 方 既 有 客 戶 重 疊 程 度 不 高。華 邦 電 NOR Flash 應
用以 PC 、 NB 市 場 為 主 ，Infineon 相 關 業 務 則 具 有 較 多 歐 美 級 車 用 客 戶 ， 有
助 公 司 收 購 後 直 接 擴 大 NOR Flash 規 模 ， 並 補 足 不 同 區 域 及 終 端 市 場 的 客
戶布局。  
此 外 ， 此 次 交 易 另 一 重 點 在 研 發 團 隊 。 收 購 標 的 具 有 逾30 年 發 展 歷 史 ， 研
發 據 點 分 布 歐 洲 、 亞 洲 、 東 南 亞 及 美 國 ， 其 中 以 美 西 團 隊 規 模 較 大。 標的公
司 Spansion 已 在 車 用 及 工 控 記 憶 體 市 場 累 積 長 期 產 品 與 市 場 經 驗 ， 收 購 後
可望使華邦電直接取得既有產品及市場布局，降低進入高階車用記憶體市
場 從 零 建 立 產 品 與 客 戶 關 係 的 時 間 。 不 過 ， 若 後 續 涉 及 製 程 或 生 產 地 調 整 ，
仍 須 依 客 戶 要 求 進 行 PCN 及 相 關 驗 證 ， 因 此 客 戶 及 產 品 整 合 效 益 仍 需 時 間
逐 步 實 現 。 
Infineon 著重 資 產 組 合 調 整 ，出售 NOR Flash 事業， 而 非 對 NOR Flash 需
求 前 景 的 否 定：

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 17 日

2 群益投顧 
本 次 交 易 較 偏 向 Infineon 的 資 產 組 合 調 整 ， 而 非 對 NOR Flash 需 求 前 景 的
否定， Infineon 自 己 目 前 仍 是 NOR Flash 第四大廠， 2025 年 市 占 約 11.5% ，
並 不 是 競 爭 力 不 佳 才 處 份 。 
Infineon 仍 保 留 多 項 特 殊 記 憶 體 產 品 ， 並 沒 有 全 面 退 出 記 憶 體 ， 公 司 特 別 說
明 交 易 完 成 後 仍 會 保 留 SRAM、HYPERRAM 、nvSRAM 及 輻 射 強 化 型 SONOS
記 憶 體 等 特 殊 記 憶 體 產 品 。 
Infineon 將 資 本 配 置 進 一 步 集 中 在 核 心 成 長 領 域 ， 重 點 包 括AI 資 料 中 心 電
源 、 車 用 功 率 半 導 體 、MCU 及 連 接 等 ； 公 司 2026 財 年 也 大 幅 提 高 AI 電源
相 關 營 收 目 標 。 
中 長 期 可 改 善 生 產 成 本：  
原 Infineon 記 憶 體 產 品 目 前 仍 有 相 當 比 例 採外 部 晶 圓 代 工 ， 華 邦 電 未 來 可
評 估 利 用 自 身 12 吋 晶 圓 產 能 進 行 部 分 產 品 內 製 ， 理 論 上 可 提 高 自 有 產 能 利
用 率 並 降 低 外 部 製 造 成 本。不 過，車 用 產 品 對 供 應 商 及 製 造 流 程 變 更 具 有 較
高 要 求 ， 若 涉 及 晶 圓 製 造 地 或 製 程 轉 換 ， 仍 須 經 過PCN 及 重 新 驗 證 ， 因 此
相 關 成 本 綜 效 屬 中 長 期 效 益 。 
預期 256Mb 及 以 上 高 容 量 產 品 在 2H26 平 均 價 格 仍 有 90 -110% 的 上 漲 空 間： 
根據 TrendForce 報 告 ， NOR Flash 市 場 正 經 歷 近 10 年 來 最 重 要 的 供 需 結 構
轉變； AI 需 求 排 擠 ， 供 應 商 NOR Flash 位 元 產 出 增 加 有 限 ， 加 上 高 階 應 用
需 求 升 級 ， 預 期 2H26 供 需 仍 舊 失 衡 ， 整 體 合 約 價 格 維 持 在 高 點 ， 高 容 量 產
品 價 格 甚 至 可 能 翻 倍 成 長 。 
供 給 端 方 面，即 便 台 系、陸 系 供 應 商 皆 已 提 高 資 本 支 出 並 擴 產，但 因 新 增 產
能 必 須 經 過 製 程 微 縮、良 率 爬 坡、產 品 驗 證，以 及 納 入 客 戶 規 格 與 系 統 設 計
(design -in) ， 無 法 立 即 滿 足 市 場 的 需 求 ， 因 此 ， 短 期 內 中 高 容 量NOR Flash
市 場 的 缺 口 仍 無 法 獲 得 滿 足 。 
NOR Flash 需 求 方 面 ， 除 傳 統 的 先 進 駕 駛 輔 助 系 統(ADAS) 、 數 位 座 艙 及 工
控 應 用 ，AI 與 航 太 通 訊 領 域 則 為 2026 年 新 增 需 求 。 以 AI 伺 服 器 為 例 ， 單
台對 NOR 的 需 求 量 為 傳 統 伺 服 器 的 3~5 倍 ， 且 AI 伺 服 器 內 部 配 備 大 量
Retimer 晶片，每顆 Retimer 均需要獨立 NOR Flash 載 入 微 碼 ， 推 升 高 容 量
及 Octal SPI NOR 需 求 。 此 外 ，Edge -AI 、 AI PC 與 機 器 人 等 本 地 推 論 設 備 帶
動 模 型 韌 體 規 模 數 倍 膨 脹，也 提 升 高 密 度 NOR Flash 需 求。低 軌 衛 星 與 航 太
電 子 領 域 方 面 ， 衛 星 本 體 的 核 心 晶 片 組 需 要 可 靠 度 高 的 NOR Flash 執行快
速 開 機 與 韌 體 備 份 ， 地 面 接 收 天 線 亦 內 建 NOR 以 維 持 即 時 運 算 與 OTA 韌
體 更 新 。 而 航 太 用 NOR 要 求 具 備 抗 輻 射 與 耐 極 端 溫 差 特 性 ， 為 台 系 、 美 系
廠 商 建 立 技 術 壁 壘 。 
TrendForce 表 示 ， 1H26 NOR Flash 合 約 價 平 均 累 積 漲 幅 達 100 -120% ，預期
2H26 不 同 容 量 產 品 價 格 走 勢 將 分 化 。256Mb 及 以 上 高 容 量 產 品 有 AI server 、
車 用 等 強 勁 需 求 ， 加 上 供 給 主 力 台 系 業 者 的 新 增 位 元 有 限 ， 預 估2H26 平均
價格仍有 90 -110% 的 上 漲 空 間 。 而 128Mb 及 以 下 的 中 低 容 量 市 場 ， 隨 著 中
系 供 應 商 產 能 逐 步 釋 出 ， 消 費 類 產 品 價 格 漲 勢 趨 緩 ， 後 續 將 進 入 高 檔 盤 整 ，
預估 4Q26 漲 幅 落 在 10 -20% 之 間 。

3 群益投顧 
華 邦 電 資 本 配 置 和 回 收 期 為 未來 觀 察 重 點 ：  
本 次 交 易 金 額 達 11.2 億美元 (約 353.92 億元 )， 交 易 標 的 為 重 組 後NOR Flash
及 F-RAM 事 業 之 100% 股 權 ， 並 不 包 含 晶 圓 廠 等 大 規 模 製 造 資 產 ， 因 此 以
交 易 金 額 觀 察 ， 價 格 並 不 低 。 
收 購 交 易 金 額 約 353.92 億 元 ， 資 金 將 以 自 有 資 金 或 融 資 活 動 支 應 。 另 一 方
面，公 司 另 規 劃 發 行 第 四 次 及 第 五 次 無 擔 保 轉 換 公 司 債，發 行 面 額 上 限 分 別
為 140 億元及 40 億 元 ， 合 計 最 高 180 億 元 ， 票 面 利 率 均 為 0% ，募得資金
主 要 用 於 產 能 擴 充、先 進 製 程 研 發 及 機 器 設 備 購 置。形 成 外 部 取 得 產 品 與 市
場、內 部 擴 充 製 造 能 力 的 資 本 配 置 方 向。惟 兩 項 計 畫 同 步 推 進，未 來 資 金 需
求 及 資 本 支 出 均 將 增 加，仍 需 觀 察 收 購 資 金 來 源、 融 資 成 本 及 CB 未 來 轉 換
所 可 能 產 生 的 股 本 稀 釋 效 果 。 
這次交易 華 邦 電 主 要 取 得 產 品 、 技 術 、IP 、 客 戶 及 全 球 營 運 資 源 ， 後 續 仍 需
透 過 產 品 擴 張、客 戶 深 化 及 製 造 資 源 整 合 逐 步 創 造 綜 效；若 實 際 獲 利 貢 獻 低
於 預 期 ， 則 可 能 使 投 資 回 收 期 拉 長 ， 並 增 加 併 購 資 本 配 置 的 壓 力 。 
華 邦 電 建 議 Buy ：  
AI 的 發 展 改 變 產 業 追 求 性 價 比 與 記 憶 體 單 價 的 傳 統 ， 轉 而 重 視 效 能 、 算 力
及 大 容 量 。 記 憶 體 市 場 供 需 出 現 結 構 性 改 變 。 進 入3Q26 DRAM 與 Flash 的
價 格 仍 維 持 上 行 趨 勢，雖 然 價 格 上 漲 趨 勢 較 緩，但 毛 利 率 仍 持 續 向 上。預 估
華邦電 2026 年 稅 後 EPS 為 24.85 元 (不 含 此 次 收 購)。除 AI 伺 服 器 需 求 外 ，
交換器及 Edge AI 應 用 亦 帶 動 NOR Flash 與 SLC NAND 需 求 持 續 成 長 ， 華
邦電憑藉 NOR Flash 全 球 市 占 率 第 一 及 SLC NAND 擴 產 ， 可 望 持 續 受 惠 。
此 外 ， 公 司 矽 電 容 目 前 產 能 接 近 滿 載 ， 受 惠AI 先 進 封 裝 需 求 快 速 成 長 ， 將
成 為 新 的 獲 利 來 源， 中長期 則 可 望 受惠 Spansion 收購 效益 ， 因此 對 華 邦 電
建議 Buy 。

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

Wistron

Reiterate Buy: Aug sales +49% MoM, aided 
by solid AI momentum; lift PO to NT$265

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 265.00 TWD | Price: 189.00 TWD

Aug’26 sales +49% MoM, mainly driven by AI 
Wistron’s Aug’26 sales of NT$460bn grew 49% MoM (+166% YoY), with subsidiary 
Wiwynn’s Aug sales of NT$144bn grew 23% MoM (+50% YoY). Wistron’s strong Aug 
sales were mainly driven by enterprise AI server projects, with overall AI server rack 
shipments roughly doubling MoM. Given the strong Aug sales, we lift our 3Q26 sales 
forecast by 11%. We expect Sep sales to decline 20% MoM to NT$370bn from the high 
Aug base. Yet, Sep sales should remain above July’s NT$308bn as we stay positive on AI 
order momentum into following quarters, driven by Nvidia’s new Vera Rubin generation.  
PC remains soft; Aug shipments down 13% MoM 
Wistron’s Aug NB shipments were 1.4mn (-13% MoM, -36% YoY). We view it as in line 
with supply chain’s commentary on weaker demand in 2H26 amid continued component 
cost inflation issue. This also echoes Wistron management’s cautious tone that PC 
shipment should decline 10-20% HoH in 2H26 following earlier order pull-in 1H26. We 
now expect 3Q26 NB shipment to fall 18% QoQ, implying 1.5mn units of NB shipments 
in Sep (+8% MoM, -31% YoY) with potential quarter-end pull-in demand. 
Lift PO to NT$265; reiterate Buy on solid AI momentum 
We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 4-6% to reflect higher sales, mainly driven by AI (with 
diversifying clients/projects), better operating leverage, and actual 2Q26 data. We raise 
our PO to NT$265 from NT$238, with our valuation method unchanged at 15x 2H26-
1H27E EPS. We reiterate Buy on Wistron’s solid multi-year AI server outlook. We expect 
a positive near-term market reaction to the strong Aug sales.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Wistron is one of the top-5 ODMs globally. Server/storage 
and PC business accounted for 73% and 24% of sales in 
2025, respectively. Key server/storage customers include 
Dell, Lenovo, HPE, Cisco, Nvidia, AMD, Intel. Key NB 
customers include Dell, Lenovo, Acer. Wistron was spun off 
from Acer (2353 TT) in 2000 and was incorporated in May 
2001. It went publicly listed on the Taiwan stock exchange 
in Aug 2003. It is headquartered in Taipei, Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to Wistron in view of improving 
sales/profit mix, solid outlook from Wiwynn, high exposure 
to server business after exiting from low-margin iPhone 
assembly business, and improving OPM. We believe 
Wistron will benefit from Dell's strong demand in Nvidia AI 
GPU server rack as the key supplier.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Wistron (WICOF) 
Our PO of NT$265 is based on 15x 2H26-1H27E EPS. We use 15x valuation multiple 
(around 1.5SD above historical P/E average since 2022) in view of high server mix, a key 
position in AI server supply chain with exposure both to component/rack business, and 
ongoing OPM improvement.

Upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger server market demand, especially for AI, (2) 
stronger PC demand, (3) share/order win in AI server business, and (4) favorable FX 
trend.

Downside risks to our PO are (1) softer AI server demand due to AI GPU short supply or 
supply chain hiccup, (2) weaker PC market demand amid inflation/macro uncertainties, 
(3) GM pressure due to higher labor/logistics cost, (4) more intensified competition in 
NB and server business, and (5) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) 
to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

## Document 090: 260910_gs_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260910_gs_TSMC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-10
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Presenters: Wendell Huang, SVP & CFO  
Bottom Line: We have three key takeaways: (1) TSMC continues to view AI as a 
multi-year megatrend that remains at a very early stage, with lower compute costs 
expected to accelerate AI adoption and ultimately drive greater demand for 
advanced silicon; (2) execution in Arizona continues to progress well, with Phase 1 
achieving yields comparable to Taiwan and already generating profits, while TSMC 
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside 
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona; and (3) despite dilution from the N2 ramp 
and overseas expansion, management remains comfortable achieving its long-term 
gross margin target of 56% and above, supported by disciplined capacity planning, 
high utilization, productivity improvements, cost reductions, and pricing that reflects 
TSMC’s value. 
Key Takeaways

AI remains in the early stages of a multi-year megatrend: TSMC continues to n
see increasing adoption of AI. Management also highlighted the shift from 
generative AI toward agentic AI, which should broaden compute demand 
beyond accelerators to CPUs. TSMC remains confident in the durability of the AI 
cycle based on discussions not only with its direct customers but also with its 
customers’ customers. Recent visits of US CSPs further reinforced management’s 
confidence, as cloud service providers appeared well prepared in areas including 
power and broader infrastructure deployment. 
Lower compute costs should drive greater consumption and silicon demand: n
Management believes open-source models and continued improvements in 
computing efficiency will reduce token costs, leading to greater AI 
consumption and broader adoption, supporting incremental demand for 
advanced silicon. TSMC has already incorporated this dynamic into its capacity 
planning and continues to expand aggressively to support what it views as

### Page 2

structural, multi-year demand growth.
Disciplined demand validation remains critical amid aggressive expansion: n
TSMC uses a combination of top-down and bottom-up analysis to validate demand.
Its top-down assessment considers macroeconomic conditions, semiconductor
demand, fabless/system companies, the foundry industry, etc, while its bottom-up
approach evaluates demand across different segments and incorporates feedback
from customers and customers’ customers. Management acknowledged that

semiconductors will remain cyclical, but believes correctly identifying the underlying
megatrend is more important than attempting to forecast every short-term
fluctuation.
Arizona execution is demonstrating that TSMC’s manufacturing model can n
travel: TSMC reiterated that overseas expansion is based on customer demand for
geographic flexibility and an appropriate level of government support. Arizona
Phase 1, using N4 technology, is in operation, with yields comparable to Taiwan,

satisfactory quality and reliability, and positive profitability. Management views
this as an important milestone because it demonstrates internally and to customers
that TSMC can produce wafers in the US with the same quality as in Taiwan.
Equipment move-in for Phase 2, which will use N3 technology, is expected to begin
soon; construction of Phase 3 has started; and preparation work for Phase 4 and the
first US advanced-packaging facility is underway.

Arizona has substantial room for further expansion: In addition to its original n
Arizona development, TSMC has acquired another parcel of land capable of
accommodating an additional five to six fabs, supporting its recently
announced incremental US$100bn investment. Based on its current plan, TSMC
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona. However, the most advanced technologies

will continue to ramp first in Taiwan because the early-stage technology transfer
requires close collaboration and frequent movement of engineers among R&D and
manufacturing sites. Overseas deployment will generally follow after the technology
has stabilized in Taiwan.
Customers increasingly value and are willing to pay for geographic flexibility: n
Management noted that customers initially requested only a modest amount of
geographically diversified supply, but their requirements have increased as TSMC has

demonstrated successful execution in Arizona. TSMC believes this geographic
flexibility represents incremental value and intends to reflect that value in its pricing,
with customers increasingly willing to recognize the associated premium.
Technology, manufacturing excellence, capacity, and trust underpin TSMC’s n
competitive moat: Management believes customers select a foundry partner based
on access to the best technology, the ability to ramp rapidly into high-volume

manufacturing with attractive economics, sufficient capacity, and confidence that
the foundry will not compete with its customers. TSMC is confident in maintaining
this lead by continuing to advance its roadmap. Management also highlighted its
record of rapidly ramping new technologies, the scale of its wafer and
advanced-packaging investments, and its consistent delivery against customer
10 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

commitments as key elements of customer trust that cannot be replicated through
CapEx or engineering recruitment alone.
Pricing remains strategic rather than opportunistic: TSMC does not intend to raise n
prices simply because near-term utilization is high, nor reduce prices simply because
utilization temporarily weakens. Instead, the company’s approach is to price
sustainably and consistently based on the value it provides, while ensuring that
customers can remain successful over the long term. Management noted that

TSMC’s structural profitability has continued to improve, with its long-term gross
margin target increasing from 50% five years ago to 53% and now 56% and above.
Leading-edge wafer and advanced-packaging capacity remain tight: n
Management continues to build capacity wherever possible to meet customer
demand. N3 demand remains particularly strong: unlike prior technology cycles,
when demand for a node typically began declining two to three years after its

introduction, TSMC is adding further N3 capacity and still does not expect supply
to be sufficient. The company is also expanding advanced-packaging capacity to
address strong accelerator demand. Outside TSMC, management identified power
and memory as potential bottlenecks cited by other parts of the AI supply chain,
while emphasizing that foundry capacity itself remains difficult to expand because
fabs require two to three years to build and another one to two years to ramp.

TSMC’s disclosed AI revenue understates its broader exposure: Management n
reiterated that its disclosed AI revenue definition is relatively narrow and primarily
captures GPUs, custom AI accelerators and memory base die. It excludes CPUs and
networking products because TSMC cannot reliably determine whether each chip is
ultimately deployed in an AI system. As agentic AI drives incremental demand across
x86, Arm, and RISC-V CPUs, as well as networking and other supporting silicon,

TSMC’s total AI-related exposure is therefore broader than its reported
AI-accelerator revenue. The company intends to retain its existing definition until it
can more accurately distinguish the end use of these products.
No visible barrier to continued transistor scaling: TSMC is currently ramping N2, n
with A14 expected approximately two years later, while development of the
subsequent node is already underway. Management does not currently see a

technical issue preventing delivery of the next node or continued advancement
beyond the existing roadmap. TSMC views close collaboration with customers as a
key foundry advantage, allowing the company to develop each technology against
specific customer performance, power, density, and economic requirements.
Cross-node capacity optimization is an underappreciated advantage: TSMC n
highlighted its ability to generate additional output through productivity

improvements and to convert tools and capacity across technology nodes. For
example, the company can use portions of its N5 capacity to support N3 demand
and can also optimize capacity across less-adjacent nodes where technically
feasible. Management views this cross-node flexibility as a distinctive element of
TSMC’s manufacturing excellence, allowing the company to respond more
quickly to demand changes while reducing the risk of capacity obsolescence.

Advanced packaging is strategic primarily because it enables wafer growth: n
10 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

CoWoS capacity remains tight, and TSMC already started outsourcing selected 
packaging processes to OSAT partners to support additional growth. Management 
emphasized that advanced packaging is intended primarily to ensure and enable 
wafer demand rather than serve as a standalone profit pool. TSMC is therefore 
developing multiple packaging architectures, including panel-level packaging, etc, 
while remaining open to customers using alternative packaging providers, as either 
approach ultimately supports demand for TSMC wafers. 
Advanced-packaging execution remains a key differentiator: TSMC noted that its n
current advanced-packaging capability supports approximately 5.5x reticle size, 
with a roadmap to reach approximately 14x reticle sizes within the next several 
years. While competing roadmaps may also appear attractive, management believes 
execution and the ability to deliver in high-volume manufacturing will ultimately 
determine competitive outcomes. TSMC does not believe competitors can use 
advanced-packaging wins to meaningfully displace its leading-edge wafer 
business, as foundry and packaging remain fundamentally different businesses. 
A unified TSMC culture supports overseas manufacturing execution: TSMC’s n
objective is not to create separate Taiwan and US cultures, but to establish a 
consistent TSMC culture centered on integrity, commitment, innovation, and 
customer trust (ICIC). Locally hired Arizona employees spend six to eighteen months 
training and working in Taiwan, while experienced Taiwan-based employees are 
assigned to Arizona to work alongside local teams. Over time, the number of Taiwan 
assignees should decline as locally trained employees assume greater responsibility. 
TSMC is applying this approach across its overseas manufacturing sites. 
Management remains comfortable with its 56%-and-above long-term gross n
margin target: TSMC reiterated its 6 major drivers of profitability: new-technology 
ramp, pricing, cost, product mix, utilization, and FX. The N2 ramp is expected to 
dilute 2026 GM by approximately 2-3ppts, but its profitability should improve over 
time toward the corporate average; management also noted that N2’s structural 
profitability is better than N3 at a comparable stage. Overseas expansion is 
expected to dilute GM by 2-3ppts during the early portion of the current five-year 
period, then widening to 3-ppts later as more overseas fabs begin production. Each 
new fab will initially create dilution before improving along its learning curve. Taking 
these puts and takes together, management remains very comfortable that 
disciplined capacity planning, healthy utilization, cost reduction, productivity 
gains, and pricing that reflects TSMC’s value will enable the company to deliver 
for shareholders and achieve its long-term gross margin target ‘of 56% and 
higher’ with focus on the ‘higher’ side. 
Valuation/Risks

TSMC (Buy): Our 12-month price target is NT$3,100, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12-month price 
target of US$620, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. Key

downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery

competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

Share prices as at: Sep 7, 2026.  Source: FactSet, Morgan Stanley Research
estimates.
We expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to be the key
beneficiaries of the new iPhone launch. We downgrade Largan
to EW and Genius to UW.  Both can benefit from the variable
aperture lens upgrade, but enthusiasm over Co-Packaged Optics

has made the stocks overvalued.
The iPhone opportunity is real for Largan and Genius…but their stocks have
rallied 48% and 85%, respectively, in the past month: (The TAIEX is up 5% over
this period.)
We now value Largan at NT$7,100/share – no longer attractive: Our analysis
suggests that Largan's current share price already embeds more than 30% global
market share in Fiber Array Units (FAU) before formal production starts in 2H27 or
even 2028. Our residual income model yields a value of NT$3,000/share for the

company's traditional business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$4,100/
share for CPO/FAU new business, bringing the total price target to NT$7,100.
However, any delay in CPO/FAU development poses significant downside risk. We
therefore downgrade to EW.
We now value Genius at NT$910/share – but  even on this basis, we view it as
overvalued: Similarly, we believe Genius's share price is also inflated by hopes for
FAU components. Using a similar approach, we conclude that aggregate value is NT

$910/share (NT$600 for existing business and NT$310/share for FAU component
business). We recommend taking profits.
Reiterate OW on Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal and AAC: We believe all four companies
will benefit from the new iPhone launch. Here, however,  we still see upside in the
stocks. We see these companies, benefiting in various ways:
• Luxshare: Key player in iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max assembly, also benefits from
variable aperture upgrade as component supplier.

•  Lingyi: Higher content value form both iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and foldable.
•  Crystal: Micro prism modules, coated filters, and cover lenses should enjoy
positive ASP and volume growth.
• AAC: Vapor chamber (VC) heat dissipation upgrade for iPhone 18 Pro Max
and Pro is the key catalyst.
Remain EW on Sunny Optical and GoerTek: Sunny is a key variable aperture
component vendor, but it's been well on track to meet the goal of doubling revenue,
implying limited upside surprise. GoerTek could face labor shortages and margin

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Executive Summary
With Largan and Genius's significant outperformance YTD, we believe the iPhone 18
launch is less likely to become a key driver of further stock price movement for them.
Instead, our analysis suggests that the CPO enthusiasm could have already made these
stocks overvalued. We therefore downgrade Largan from OW to  EW and downgrade
Genius from EW  to UW.
Meanwhile, we expect Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC to become the key beneficiaries

amid iPhone new product launches. These stocks haven't rallied materially, and we see
upside potential in them.
We maintain EW ratings on Sunny and GoerTek and expect limited positive surprise from
the iPhone peak season.
Exhibit 3: Downgrade Largan to EW and  Genius  to UW, Reiterate OW on Luxshare, Lingyi, Crystal, and AAC

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Implications from iPhone 18 Launch for Various Supply
Chain Companies
Camera spec upgrade is a positive earnings driver, but CPO
enthusiasm has made Largan and Genius overvalued
The iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max have applied a variable aperture main camera for the first
time, representing a significant hardware upgrade.  The ASP of  variable aperture lenses will
be higher than it is for traditional lenses. Thus, a rapid shipment increase in 3Q26 could

become a key re-rating catalyst – indeed, it has already prompted sharp re-rating for
Largan, Genius, and Sunny.
Besides this, the launch of Apple's first foldable iPhone will also provide an incremental
demand driver beyond the standard iPhone 18 lineup. According to Nikkei Asia (link),
foldable smartphone orders increased from 8 million units to 10 million units this year,
and if the new product performs well in 4Q2026, it could become a positive catalyst for

Apple supply chain companies.
Beyond variable aperture and foldable, the further development of a new periscope lens
with the key customer is likely to expand  these use of periscope lenses in the future. This
could also become a positive driver for optical lens companies in the supply chain.
1. Largan: iPhone opportunity is real, but recent enthusiasm
over CPO made valuation too aggressive – downgrade to EW
Largan's revenue follows a well-established seasonal pattern tied to Apple's annual iPhone

launch cycle. After shipments declined continuously from March 2026 through June 2026,
we expect a seasonal recovery to begin in 3Q26, with sequential increases highly likely in
the following months. Largan's August 2026 consolidated revenue reached NT$5.012
billion, up 3% month-over-month and its best performance in four months, and the
company expects September's shipment momentum to surpass August's.
Largan is a key supplier of variable aperture lenses for the iPhone 18 Pro series, and the

company could also benefit from foldable and periscope opportunities, we raise our
intrinsic value estimate of the company's traditional business from NT$2,814/share to NT
$3,000/share. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 18.
Besides the Apple lens opportunity, we believe Largan's share performance is also highly
correlated to CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO
opportunity, we undertook the following analysis:

1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
7.78mn in 2027,with a further surge to 48.6mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU (Fiber
Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple optical
fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics) volume for
CPO also grows from 0.39 to 48.6 mn for the same period of time;
2. We assume ASP of US$100 in 2026-2028 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
2027 and 40% in 2028 (previously 20%).
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$4mn in 2027 and US$579 mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, and
considering the first year of scalable production), it could bring incremental market cap of
US$20.278bn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% rate, we derive a per-share positive
impact of NT$4,100.

We hold a positive view on Largan's CPO/FAU business opportunity because the company
has a superior track record of high precision manufacturing relative to most industry peers
in Taiwan. However, our calculation above suggests that the current share price already
reflects Largan's potential  to attain >30% global market share for FAU products.
Meanwhile, formal production hasn't kicked off yet – it is more likely to start in the

second half of 2027 or even 2028. Therefore, any delay in CPO/FAU development could
result in significant share price fluctuation. Considering current risk-reward, even after
raising our price target 47%, we no longer view Largan as attractive any more in view of
the recent strong share price performance. We set our price target at NT$7,100, within
which NT$3,000 is from its traditional business and NT$4,100 is from CPO/FAU new
business.
2. Genius: Thesis is similar to Largan's –  CPO opportunities

also overpriced, downgrade to UW
Genius, as the second-largest optical lens vendor to Apple, is also well positioned to
benefit from the camera spec upgrade highlighted in the previous section. Considering the
growth opportunities discussed above, we raise our intrinsic value estimate for the

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
existing business from NT$425 to NT$600. For more details, please refer to Exhibit 17.
Similar to Largan, we believe Genius' share price performance is also highly correlated to
CPO development. In order to assess the fundamental value from the CPO opportunity,
we undertook the following analysis:
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall

solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and
30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$54mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple vs. Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US

$1,322mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$310.

Similar to Largan, our residual income model derives intrinsic value of NT$600/share for
the traditional business, while the TAM analysis suggests CPO/FAU-related opportunities
could be valued at NT$310/share. Adding these together, we raise our price target

materially to NT$910, but that still implies 14% downside from the latest closing price.
3. Sunny: Apple revenue to double in 2026, but positive is
well known
We believe Sunny Optical will benefit from the new iPhone launch because:
1. The company is a key vendor of  variable aperture actuators – a key spec upgrade

### Page 8

M
Idea
8
with higher ASP.
2. Sunny is expanding the product mix from a single category to multiple categories,
which could generate higher revenue from the key client.
3. Sunny is also gaining share in high-end lens products.
These drivers collectively underpin management's guidance of doubling  Apple revenue for
the full-year of 2026.
However, the fundamental performance is in line with management's guidance, so there is
lack of potential positive surprise, in our view. We therefore raise our net income

estimates 29.6% for 2026 and 26.5% for 2027 based on upbeat 1H26 results.
Consequently, we raise our price target to HK$75 but maintain our EW rating –  the strong
growth from Apple has been well expected.
4. Crystal: Prisms, coated filters, and cover lenses to benefit
We expect Crystal Optech to benefit from Apple's new product launch because  the
company is supplying three key products in the supply chain:
1. Its micro prism modules are a key enabling component for periscope (folded-

optics) telephoto cameras in smartphones. The company's prism technology uses a
folded optical path to achieve long-focal-length telephoto capability without
increasing device thickness. In 1H26, the micro prism module business enjoyed
significant volume growth. The iPhone 18 launch will directly extend this demand
into 2H26.
2. Crystal-Optech's coated filter business — which brings micro-nano optical
processing into the IRCF manufacturing process to significantly improve petal

ghosting and chromatic aberration in special imaging scenarios — is another key
segment that benefits from the iPhone 18 launch. Apple's high-end Pro models are
the primary application for this product, so the company is highly likely to
generate rapid revenue and earnings growth.
3. The iPhone 18 Pro models are expected to feature advances in 3D sensing,
including a smaller Dynamic Island as some Face ID components move beneath the
display, which sustains demand for these optical components. Crystal Optech is

well positioned to capture the growth opportunity in iPhone's 3D sensing and
cover lenses.
The company's 2Q26 results were a bit weaker than we expected because of rising costs
from R&D and capacity expansion related to new products. However, we believe this
investment could lead to stronger growth in 2028 and onward. As a result, we trim our
earnings estimates 15.6% for 2026 and 2.9% for 2027 but raise our 2028 estimate 8.2%.
Consequently, we raise our price target  7%, to Rmb32, implying 24% upside from the

current level.
Luxshare: Apple opportunity underappreciated,  resilient
growth intact
We believe Luxshare's Apple opportunity is underappreciated –  some investors worry
about the supply chain relocation. However, the company is one of Apple's most deeply

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
embedded manufacturing partners, covering various products and components, as
illustrated in the chart below. The company has  also proved its operational resilience,
consistently registering positive growth in the Apple business.
Exhibit 7: Luxshare: One of the most deeply embedded manufacturing partners for Apple
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
We expect Luxshare to benefit from the latest new product  launch through the following

four key drivers:
1.  Second largest assembler for iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max — the most premium-heavy
iPhone lineup in history. If the  Pro/Pro Max models can take 70%+ of total iPhone
volume, it could mark the highest high-end model contribution over the past
several model cycles. A higher share from high-end models implies better revenue
and margin outlook for Luxshare.
2.   Capturing component opportunity in new  variable aperture actuator supply.

Instead of doing assembly, Lxushare is also playing the role  of supplier of actuator
mechanisms for the variable aperture camera system in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro
Max. This helps Luxshare gain exposure to high-end component business, which
carries higher margin than assembly business.
3. Foldable iPhone (iPhone Ultra) exposure: While Luxshare is not the assembler for
the foldable phone, it has actively developed foldable hinge technology, with R&D

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
focused on high-precision, long-life, large-angle hinge modules for inward-folding
flexible-display devices. The company is also developing ultra-thin haptic motors
tailored for foldable smartphones. When foldable shipments reach a certain scale,
Luxshare is also highly likely to become the second assembler.
4.  Leading AirPods assembly share:  In the AirPods assembly market, we expect
Luxshare to remain the largest supplier, with GoerTek as the second player. The

iPhone 18 launch is accompanied by new AirPods models, and Luxshare's dominant
AirPods assembly position means that it captures the full breadth of Apple's 2H26
product cycle — not just the iPhone.
Despite a significant FX loss, Luxshare posted resilient earnings in 1H26. We nudge our
EPS estimates up 0.2% each for 2026 and 2027 and 2.0% for 2028 to reflect the solid
1H26 results and positive growth outlook in the following 2-3 years. As a result, we also

raise our H-share price target to HK$82/share, implying 34% upside potential.
Lingyi: Peak season to drive strong earnings improvement
Lingyi  provides a broad range of components, including core precision parts, hinge
precision structural components, screen support plates, flanged ultra-thin stainless steel
battery case modules, etc. Following the launch of iPhone 18 Pro, Pro Max, and Apple's
first-ever foldable iPhone, Lingyi is likely to benefit from  higher content per unit on

standard Pro models, and a step-change in content value from the foldable iPhone.
Exhibit 8: Lingyi: Higher content value from new iPhone
Source: Company Data, Morgan Stanley Research
Lingyi also has good exposure to the iPhone  foldable product, with content value per
foldable device expected by the company to reach US$80–90, including titanium inner
hinges, hinge components, and mid-frame support structures — substantially above the
content value of conventional smartphones.

Considering the positive drivers in 3Q26, we expect Lingyi to achieve significant earnings
improvement on a QoQ basis, which could become a key re-rating catalyst.
We slightly adjust our net income  estimates after 1H26: up 1.6%  in 2026, 0.3% and 1.7% in
2027 and 2028. However, our price targets for both the H- and A-shares are unchanged
because the revision is minor while our long-term RI model assumptions remain

Morgan Stanley Research 11
unchanged.
AAC: Key beneficiary of  iPhone VC application
AAC Tech is one of Apple's key component suppliers, providing a broad portfolio spanning
acoustics, haptics, and thermal management. The iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and iPhone Ultra
launch is a direct, multi-dimensional catalyst across all those business segments.
Within the three segments, we believe thermal management –  Vapor Chamber (VC) Heat
Dissipation – is the most important catalyst. AAC Technologies is a key vendor of VC heat

spreaders in the iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max. The significant expansion of the VC cooling
area is driven by the iPhone 18 Pro's A20 Pro chip, which debuts on a 2nm process node
with complex WMCM packaging technology to support on-device AI computation, leading
to increased thermal output. Apple first introduced VC liquid cooling in the iPhone 17 Pro
series, with AAC as the primary supplier for that generation as well.
This division has already demonstrated significant growth momentum heading into the

iPhone 18 ramp up. In 1H26, heat dissipation revenue surged approximately 400% YoY, to
RMB1.10 billion, primarily driven by the continued ramp-up in shipments of high-
performance innovative vapor chambers. The company expects VC shipments to exceed
180,000k units in 2026, up from 140,000k+  units in 2025.

Combining the strong growth momentum in thermal management, but weaker-than-
expected performance in the optics segment (all supplied to Android), we raise our
earnings estimates  8.4% for 2026 and 6.0% for 2027. After 1H26 results, we believe
investors are aware of the weakness in optics, while the iPhone launch could help re-rate

the stock. We raise our price target to HK$50 – the stronger 19% increase reflects
earnings upgrades and a higher valuation for the thermal-management business – and
maintain our OW rating.
BYDE: Normal peak season, but lackluster with limited spec
upgrade

### Page 12

M
Idea
12
While Apple is BYDE's key customer, accounting for >50% of total revenue, it is mainly
from the  iPad assembly business. Therefore, this division is less likely to benefit from the
iPhone 18 launch.
The company is also the second-largest iPhone metal casing vendor, supplying precision
structural components including titanium and aluminium alloy chassis. While iPhone 18
launch is the direct driver for this segment, the titanium-to-aluminium downgrade has

become a near-term headwind.  Titanium is a higher-value, higher-margin material for BYDE
while aluminium carries lower ASP and lower gross margin. This change has made BYDE
less likely to benefit from the iPhone 18 launch.
Despite the lackluster outlook, we expect earnings to show sequential improvement in
3Q26, driven by the normal peak season in 3Q26, followed by stronger growth in 4Q26 as
liquid cooling products for AI infrastructure also ramp up. Our earnings estimates and

price target remain unchanged.
GoerTek: Margin might face pressures despite peak season
GoerTek's primary Apple exposure is in smart acoustic complete units (AirPods assembly
and components) and precision components. We believe the  iPhone 18 launch and peak
season ramp in 3Q–4Q will drive higher shipment volumes for these products.
However, GoerTek's transfer of production  to Vietnam — where it has invested
approximately US$1.3 billion through multiple entities — has created a persistent labor

cost headwind. In 2H25, a labor shortage caused by the rapid capacity transfer forced
GoerTek to offer higher wages, triggering material  gross margin pressure  for the smart
acoustic assembly business. Overall gross margins partially recovered to 13.8% in 1Q26
and 15.2% in 2Q26, but the underlying trend of rising wages in Vietnam remains a
structural factor. As GoerTek ramps production for the iPhone 18 peak season in 3Q and
4Q, the higher labor cost base in Vietnam will continue to weigh on assembly margins.

We raise our 2026 and 2027 earnings estimates 14.9% and 10.6%, respectively, mainly
reflecting the better-than-expected gross margin recovery in 1H26. However, we raise our
price target only modestly: 4%. to Rmb27, as persistent labor-cost pressure in Vietnam
could limit further margin expansion and reduce the earnings benefit from higher peak-
season volumes.
Considering the factors discussed above, we believe GoerTek is less likely to benefit from

the new iPhone product launch and peak season volume increase.

believe these companies are key beneficiaries of the iPhone 18 launch. For details, please

Where we could be wrong
While we expect iPhone 18 to be  a positive catalyst in 2H26, we are watching three key
variables on the potential inflection points:
1.  Memory prices: These are a key variable this year. If there is a memory price
correction anytime during 2026, investors are likely to expect an earlier
smartphone shipment and margin recovery, which could reverse the negative

sentiment.
2.  LLMs: We see LLMs as a revolutionary innovation. They could introduce numerous
creative features in smartphone applications and change user habits in the years to
come. If the Edge AI breakthrough arrives earlier than we expect, it could
accelerate the smartphone product cycle and make the recovery earlier.
3. iPhone sales performance: Better-than-expected sell-through of the iPhone 18
Pro, Pro Max and its first foldable model could drive shipment upside, higher

content value and stronger capacity utilization. Conversely, weaker demand could
lead to order cuts after the initial ramp up and reduce the earnings benefits from
key hardware upgrades.

Price target changes
We derive our price targets (also our base case values) using a residual income model –
the same methodology we use for our Greater China Tech coverage.
Our new price targets are derived from revised earnings estimates and unchanged cost of
equity assumptions (RFR, beta, risk premium) and growth rates.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 098: 260914_daiwa_aspeed

Source PDF: all report pdf/260914_daiwa_aspeed.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our update on Aspeed following its upbeat 4Q26 
revenue guidance on 4 September. We believe the strong guidance 
reaffirms our positive view that agentic AI has served as a catalyst for end 
demand for regular servers starting early 2026. Baseboard management 
controller (BMC) shipments for general servers (AI and non-AI together) 
should grow c.50% YoY in 2026. We believe that the new 4Q26 revenue 
guidance implies regular server shipment volume will increase c.50% YoY 
in 2027, hence the 36/34% uplift in our 2027/28E earnings forecasts.

What’s the impact: Solid regular server shipments ahead. Aspeed’s 
latest guidance range for its 4Q26 sales at TWD6.6-6.8bn implies 4Q26 
BMC shipments of c.12m. With this run-rate, total BMC shipments in 2027 
should be near c.50m, much higher than our previous estimate of 36.1m. 
We attribute the ongoing strength to agentic AI and AI-related general-
purpose demand. We forecast Aspeed’s BMC for general servers to 
increase to 27m in 2027E (from 18m in 2026E and 13m in 2025). At the 
same time, we expect BMC for AI servers to increase c.50% to 20m in 
2027E (up from 13m in 2026E and 5m in 2025). To sum up, we now 
forecast BMC shipments of 32.7m in 2026 and 47.6m in 2027 (+46% YoY 
in 2027). Based on our AI-related general server assumptions (excluding 
non-AI general servers), we expect the CPU:XPU ratio to rise to 1:1.3 in 
2027E (from 1:2.6 in 2026E). We expect a rising CPU:XPU ratio to become 
a key growth driver and share-price catalyst in the years after 2027. Due to 
the solid end demand, management is considering adjusting its pricing 
again from November to factor in the additional packaging cost, leading to 
our higher gross margin assumptions from 4Q26.

New growth opportunities. Following our visits to Aspeed’s booth at 
Computex 2026 and the OCP APAC conference, we believe Aspeed will 
continually maximise the dollar content per server board. Compared with 
last year’s version (embedded software-defined FPGA), Aspeed is 
integrating a Lattice FPGA into its next-generation Bridge IC (SMC; 
AST1840), which should support higher ASPs for its products.

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 9-36% to factor in our 
higher BMC shipment assumptions. We lift our 12M TP to TWD26,660 
(from TWD19,800), based on an unchanged PER of 66x (high end of its 
past-3-year PER range of 21-78x), applied to our 1-year forward EPS 
forecast. Our 2025-28E EPS CAGR of 83% makes our target PER 
undemanding, given the resulting PEG ratio of 0.8. Key downside risk: 
weak AI server demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 6-26% higher than the consensus, 
likely due to our bullish view on Aspeed’s ASP and shipment story.

14 September 2026

Promising outlook on agentic AI

➢ Rising CPU:XPU ratio is becoming a key growth driver for Aspeed… 
➢ … which indicates solid regular server demand despite supply hiccups 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12-month TP to TWD26,660

Company profile 
Established in November 2004, Aspeed is a fabless IC design company specialising in baseboard 
management controllers (BMCs), which are mostly used for the remote management of servers. Aspeed 
is a global leader in BMC, with about 70% market share. Its other products include PC/AV extension 
SoC, which supports the extension of digital signals of PCs or multimedia content to remote terminals; 
and Cupola360, which is a spherical image processor that supports 360-degree cameras.

### Page 5

5

ASPEED Technology (5274 TT): 14 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 14 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Aspeed’sboard consists of 9 directors, with 4 independent directors. The directors’backgrounds
appear to have a high correlation to semiconductors (either education or work experience),
which we see as a positive for executive quality. In 2025, Aspeed held 6 board meetings and the
average attendee rate was 100%. Given Aspeed’sChairman is also the CEO, we think it is a
negative factor for board/executive management as well as external shareholders.
Capital management 1
Aspeed declared a DPS of TWD80 in 2025, representing an 77% payout ratio. Being a leading
global board management controller (BMC) IC design vendor with c.70% market share globally,
the company’sleading position and asset-light model (major expenses are from R&D
employees) allow it to maintain high ROE/ROA ratios and a high dividend payout policy. BMC IC
recently penetrated new applications (such as power supply, storage, edge servers), which can
improve its profitability on a higher revenue scale, in our view.
Related party &
transaction 2
In 2025, there were no sales to or purchases from related parties. In previous years, the main
purchases from related parties was from Avago. After selling its fully-owned Emulex unit to
Aspeed, Avago (Broadcom) became an Aspeed's shareholder with a c.6% stake in 2016. We
believe the bargaining power from its 6% shareholding was not sufficient to preload its IC
products on Aspeed's products or earn unfair profits. In any case, this relationship ended in
February 2021. As such, we see limited risk from related-party transactions.
S Supply chain
management 1
Aspeed ensures that suppliers comply with social responsibility, green environmental protection,
occupational safety and health, human rights and RBA, and prefer to work with ISO9001,
ISO14001 and ISO45001 certified suppliers. In addition, every Aspeed supplier is required to
issue a publicly disclosed sustainability management report.
E Water & wastewater
management 1
Being a fabless IC design company, Aspeed’sbusiness doesn’tinvolve a production process.
According to the company's data, it produced 4,035 tonnes of wastewater in 2025. As such, we
see limited risk from its water and waste water management.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
Being a fabless IC design company, Aspeed’sbusiness doesn’tinvolve production processes
that may produce hazardous materials. That said, the company indicated it would employ green
design concepts and simplified product structures throughout its R&D process in order to reduce
pollution. As such, we see limited risk from its waste and hazardous materials management.
E GHG emissions 1
Due to its fabless business model, Aspeed produces limited emissions of GHG, wastewater, and
waste materials each year. According to the company’sdata, it produced around 273.5 tonnes of
CO2 in 2025.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 100: 260914_gs_repon

Source PDF: all report pdf/260914_gs_repon.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-14
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

We hosted Repon’s management at our Asia Leaders Conference 2026 (Aug 31 - Sep 
2) in Hong Kong. Management remains positive on their servers rail kits business, 
driven by both general servers and AI servers, riding on the generative AI trend. The 
company’s positive tone echoes our positive view on AI servers supply chain, on 
which we raised our AI servers implied AI chips forecast by 14% / 22% / 14% in 
2026E-28E in June (report link) to +75% / 42% / 20% YoY in 2026E-28E, or 19m / 
27m / 32m in 2026E-28E. Capex estimates on leading US CSPs was also raised by 8% 
/ 27% / 25% in 2026E-28E to +76% / +35% / +8% YoY in 2026E-28E, or up to 
US$1.1tn by 2028E (report link). As we highlighted in our FII Chairman and Innolight 
Chairman visits takeaways, supply chain remains positive on rising AI investments in 
coming years, with spending across CSP , NeoCloud, Government, and Enterprise, and 
we are at an early stage of infrastructure spending and application development. 
End-customers are involved more in supply chain product development and capacity 
investment, also better reflecting real demand.  
Company profile: Repon (6584.TWO, not covered) is a leading rail kit supplier for AI 
servers, general servers, and home appliances. The company provides 2U rail kits for 
server clients, and has been gradually expanding its product line-up to 4U / 7U rail 
kits. As the early entrant of server rail kits, Repon has built deep partnerships with 
US CSP clients by offering customized services. 
Key Takeaways

1. General servers rail kits outlook: Management stated servers revenues 
contribution came down in 2Q26 vs. 1Q26 due to raw material shortage affecting 
customers’ procurement on rail kits for general servers; however, management sees 
their major CSP customer’s procurement recovering in 3Q26, and the next largest 
customer (also a leading US CSP) procurement is also recovering in September. The 
rail kits for next generation of general servers (powered by Intel’s Birch Stream 
platform) will also start shipment in 3Q26, supporting the revenues growth. 
2. AI servers rail kits outlook: Management remains positive and stated their rail

### Page 2

kits for the next generation AI server racks powered by NVIDIA is entering shipment in
late 3Q26, so is the rail kits for the next generation ASIC AI servers (air-cooling version)
for their major customer, and the rail kits for the liquid-cooling version should also start
shipment in 4Q26 with a majority ramp up in 1Q27. The rail kits for storage servers could
also show contribution in 1Q27, followed by rail kits for more ASIC AI servers, supporting

the growth ahead.

## Document 111: 260915_ms_MLCC

Source PDF: all report pdf/260915_ms_MLCC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-15
Pages: 43
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

M
Japan Insight
2
Investment Perspective
MLCC is early cycle in AI server & rack penetration
There is immense change underway for the MLCC industry with an investment narrative
that is changing as AI chips/server demand is increasingly driving MLCC scarcity.  AI is
shifting from semiconductors to MLCCs, creating a supply/demand mismatch in high-end
components. The high-end AI MLCC market is effectively a Japanese-Korean duopoly with
Murata and SEMCO together hold around 85% share. High-capacitance, low-ESL parts for

AI accelerators require hundreds to over a thousand dielectric layers with near-zero
defects and face strict customer qualification — the barrier preventing a repeat of 2017-
18, when shortages invited entry and the cycle self-corrected. AI platforms now compete
directly with consumer, automotive, and industrial applications for advanced materials,
heightening supply risk for MLCC traditional customers. We are still in the early innings,
and supply concerns are extending beyond near-term shortages to longer-term supply

security. As a result, we are seeing an increasing number of long-term supply agreements
being signed between suppliers and customers.
Exhibit 1: Sequencing the technology cycle – still early cycle for MLCC
Source:  Morgan Stanley Research estimates
Turning point in MLCC pricing
While Murata Mfg.’s CFO and Executive Deputy President Masanori Minamide stated in a
Sep 14 Nikkei interview that rising production loads for high-end products are making it

increasingly difficult to fully supply low-end products, and that the priority of defending
low-end market share has effectively declined, we believe the company’s fundamental
policy of not changing prices based solely on short-term supply-demand conditions
remains intact. That said, in our view, Murata has been gradually shifting its strategy
toward focusing more on its areas of strength and adopting pricing that better reflects the
need to cover large-scale capital investments, particularly in those core segments where it

enjoys a competitive advantage. We believe that Murata’s reluctance to enter into L TAs for
AI/data-center use value added MLCCs reflects its view that locking in prices today may
not be optimal, given the potential for MLCC pricing to rise in the future.
We expect sales growth to continue for small-sized, high-
capacity, high-value-added MLCCs for AI servers/DCs

### Page 3

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 3
MLCCs are a key business, accounting for 50% of sales at Murata, 46% at SEMCO, 19% at
Yageo, and 70% at Taiyo Yuden in 2025. We expect global MLCC shipment value to
expand at a CAGR of 20.3%, from $18.51bn in 2025 to $44.45bn in 2031. We believe
growth is set to accelerate from the 5.0% CAGR recorded in global MLCC shipment value
over the 13 years of 2012-25 because we expect continued growth in sales of small, high-

capacity, high-value-added MLCCs for AI servers/datacenters (DCs) from 2026. For AI
servers/DCs alone, we forecast MLCC shipment value to reach $23.33bn by 2031 (vs. our
previous estimate of $10.80bn), implying a 57.1% CAGR (vs. 38.1%).
Howard Kao (our Greater China Technology Hardware analyst) expects MLCC demand
volume for VR200 NVL72 racks to increase about 80% versus GB300 NVL72 racks, and
forecasts the number of MLCCs mounted in each Rubin NVL72 rack will jump to roughly

570,000 units from around 320,000 for GB300 NVL72 racks, with MLCC dollar content
per rack estimated to increase by 166%.　We expect the rapid expansion of the high-value-
added MLCC market for AI servers/DCs to be especially positive for  earnings at Murata (#1
market share in MLCCs for 2025 at 41%) and SEMCO (#2 at 23%).

Key roles of MLCCs in AI/DC
MLCCs play a role in locally stabilizing power supply (instantaneous current supply plus

noise removal) near semiconductors, such as GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. Since GPUs, CPUs,
and FPGAs repeatedly switch on and off on a nanosecond timescale, they demand large
currents instantaneously, and MLCCs meet this instantaneous current demand by
immediately discharging their stored charge. As semiconductor miniaturization advances,
circuit operating speed increases and drive voltage falls. As voltage decreases, even small
voltage fluctuations risk causing semiconductor malfunction, so even more stable power

supply voltage is required than before. Demand for capacitor capacitance is also increasing
in line with AI servers' rising computing power and power consumption, and because
board area and mountable space are limited, we expect demand for smaller, higher-
capacity MLCCs to persist.
Sharp dips vs. share price peaks provide re-entry opportunity
Year-to-date to Sep 11, share prices of the four companies had risen 127% for Murata,
449% for SEMCO, 141% for Yageo, and 160% for Taiyo Yuden. However, from their

respective June-July peaks, these have fallen 40% for Murata, 38% for SEMCO, 52% for

### Page 4

M
Japan Insight
4
Yageo, and 59% for Taiyo Yuden. Considering the strong likelihood that high-value-added
products for AI servers/DCs will continue to drive growth in MLCC demand not only in
2026 but also from 2027 onward, and that each company's earnings will stay on an
expansionary trend, we believe the conditions are now in place to consider re-entering
these stocks.

Earnings and share price risks
We expect capital expenditure on AI/DC to keep expanding and generational transitions to

### Page 6

M
Japan Insight
6
continue in GPUs, sustaining growth in demand for small, high-capacity, high-value-added
MLCCs. Risks to the continuity of AI/DC capex expansion include: (1) rising risk from the
growing practice of hyperscalers and major semiconductor companies charging usage fees
in exchange for equity investments in AI development companies, conditional on the use
of their own cloud services or semiconductors; and (2) risk that AI/DC capex growth slows

due to bottlenecks, such as power infrastructure constraints. If the pace of AI/DC capex
growth slows, we cannot rule out that MLCC demand falls short of our base case outlook
for demand. Also, as shown in Exhibits Exhibit 8 Exhibit 8  and Exhibit 9 , during the IT
bubble around 2000, there was a strong correlation between the YoY change in Murata's
and Taiyo Yuden's monthly order intake and their market cap. Since order intake has risen
sharply since Jul-Sep 2025, we cannot ignore the possibility that YoY growth in order

intake will slow. On the other hand, MLCC demand continues to expand for high-value-
added products for AI/DC, and we expect the major MLCC makers' sales to grow along
with an improving product mix.

High-value-added products for AI servers will likely drive
MLCC market growth
We expect global MLCC shipment value to increase at 20.3%
CAGR  through 2031
As shown in Exhibit 10 ,  we estimate that global MLCC shipment value fell for two
consecutive years, from $17.47bn in 2021 (up 26.0% YoY) to $14.32bn in 2022 (down
18.1%) and $12.64bn in 2023 (down 11.7%), before rising back up to $13.30bn in 2024 (up
5.2%) and $14.67bn in 2025 (up 10.3%). We increase our forecasts for shipment value from

$18.48bn to $18.51bn for 2026 (up 26.2% YoY), from $22.16bn to $24.52bn for 2027 (up
32.4%), and from $25.73bn to $28.60bn for 2028 (up 16.7%), and we expect the market to
reach $44.45bn in 2031, growing at a CAGR of 20.3% over the six-year period.

AI/DC MLCC market to continue growing at a rapid pace
We expect growth in demand to accelerate for high-value-added products for AI/DC from

the second half of 2026. Howard Kao  expects MLCC demand volume for VR200 NVL72
racks to increase about 80% versus GB300 NVL72 racks, and forecasts the number of
MLCCs mounted in each Rubin NVL72 rack will rise sharply to roughly 570,000 units from
around 320,000 units for GB300 NVL72 racks (Exhibit 11 ). This increase stems from a
rise in the number of MLCCs mounted on both compute boards and switch boards, as well
as an increase in PCB content across the overall system, including modules such as CX9

Orchid. Howard Kao estimates this increase in MLCC mounting volume will lift the dollar-
based MLCC content per rack by 166% ( Exhibit 12 ).

Role of MLCCs in AI servers
Capacitors, including multi-layer ceramic capacitors (MLCCs), function as a “dam” that
temporarily stores electricity, and are an essential electronic component for noise removal

and voltage stabilization in virtually all electronic circuits, including those used in
computers, smartphones, and automobiles. MLCCs play a role in locally stabilizing power
supply (instantaneous current supply plus noise removal) near semiconductors such as
GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. Since GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs repeatedly switch on and off on a
nanosecond timescale, they demand large currents instantaneously, and MLCCs meet this
instantaneous current demand by immediately discharging their stored charge. At the

same time, MLCCs bypass only the AC component (noise and fluctuating current), which
also serves to prevent noise from entering GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. MLCCs feature low
ESR (low resistance) and low ESL (low inductance), allowing them to track even

### Page 10

M
Japan Insight
10
nanosecond-level current fluctuations in semiconductors such as GPUs, CPUs, and FPGAs. 
MLCCs are often placed around components such as GPUs and ASICs that operate at low 
voltages of around 1.0V. As semiconductor miniaturization advances, circuit operating 
speed increases and drive voltage falls. As voltage decreases, even small voltage 
fluctuations risk causing semiconductor malfunction, requiring an even more stable power 
supply voltage than before. Demand for capacitor capacitance will also rise along with AI 
servers' improving computing power and increasing power consumption, and because 
board area and mountable space are limited, demand for smaller, higher-capacity MLCCs 
will likely continue. 
MLCC miniaturization and higher capacity to accelerate with 
VR200 NVL72
We assume that the space available for mounting MLCCs on VR200 NVL72 will not 
change significantly from the GB300 NVL72 rack, and that the transition from GB300 to 
VR200 will require installing higher-capacity MLCCs within the limited space of 
semiconductor ABF package substrates and AI accelerator boards, accelerating growth in 
demand for small-sized, high-capacity MLCCs. Murata has consistently been a leader in 
MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases, beginning mass production of 1005-size 1 F 
MLCCs in 2000 and 0402-size 1 F and 1005-size 22 F MLCCs in 2020. We expect 
demand for 1608-size 100 F, 1005-size 47 F, and 0603-size 10 F MLCCs to increase 
substantially with VR200 NVL72. We estimate that 1608-size 100 F MLCCs command a 
unit price 5-10x higher than 2012-size 100 F or 1608-size 47 F MLCCs, and we expect 
the shift to smaller, higher-capacity MLCCs accompanying the transition to VR200 NVL72 
to drive a substantial rise in ASP.

High-capacitance MLCCs (47µF+) are likely to capture a disproportionate share of this 
incremental opportunity: Our analysis suggests that 47µF+ MLCCs could account for 
approximately 50-60% of the incremental Cloud AI MLCC TAM in CY27, representing an 
additional ~US$2B market opportunity alone. This reflects both the increasing use of high-

### Page 11

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 11
capacitance devices within AI accelerator systems and a growing mix shift toward higher-
value MLCC content.
From a volume perspective, we estimate Cloud AI demand for 47µF+ MLCCs could 
increase from approximately 4 billion units in CY25 to 40B+ units by CY27: Such a 
rapid increase would represent a meaningful step-up in industry demand and could require 
substantial capacity reallocation or expansion among leading suppliers.
Based on our industry discussions, the market for high-capacitance MLCCs remains 
concentrated among a limited number of suppliers, primarily Japanese and Korean 
manufacturers, including Murata, Taiyo Yuden, and Samsung Electro-Mechanics (SEMCO). 
As demand from AI infrastructure accelerates, we believe capacity utilization for these 
products could tighten, raising the possibility of capacity crowding-out effects across 
other end markets and applications.

Materials development and production technology behind
MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases
In MLCCs, smaller sizes and higher capacities have mainly been achieved by thinning the
dielectric and electrode layers (layer thinning) and increasing the number of layers
stacked (multilayering). For high-capacity products for AI/DC, in addition to the layer
thinning and multilayering described above, efforts are accelerating to further miniaturize

and increase capacity by expanding the area of the dielectric and internal electrodes per
dielectric sheet. We believe MLCC miniaturization and capacity increases depend on: (1)
developing superior dielectric and electrode materials; (2) manufacturing highly uniform,
highly reliable slurry; (3) technology for precisely　stacking dielectric sheets; and (4)
technology for firing in a controlled atmosphere without oxidation. For high-capacity
products for AI/DC, we see the key factors as (1) materials development that increases the

crystallinity of dielectric materials while making them finer, in order to thin each dielectric
layer as much as possible (layer thinning), together with (2) manufacturing technology to
precisely stack the dielectric sheets.
