Dielectric material manufacturing process
Dielectric materials are manufactured by adding a binder, solvent, and additives to barium
titanate (BaTiO3), which is produced from barium carbonate (B aCO3) and titanium oxide

(TiO2). Methods for manufacturing barium titanate are broadly divided into the solid-
phase method and the liquid-phase method. In the solid-phase method, barium carbonate
and titanium oxide are mixed and fired at high temperature, producing barium titanate
through a chemical reaction at the firing stage. In the liquid-phase method, barium
carbonate and titanium oxide are dissolved in a solution, reacted at the solution stage, and
fired at a relatively low temperature to produce the compound. Manufacturing methods

used in mass production via the liquid-phase method include the oxalate method and the
hydrothermal synthesis method. Barium titanate with small and uniform particle size is
key to smaller and higher-capacity MLCCs. In 2023, Murata established MF Materials, a
joint venture with Ishihara Sangyo and Ishihara Sangyo's wholly owned subsidiary Fuji
Titanium (with equity stakes of 20% for Murata, 10% for Ishihara Sangyo, and 70% for Fuji
Titanium). MF Materials produces barium titanate via the oxalate process and supplies it

to Murata Manufacturing.　MF Materials is constructing a new building adjacent to its head
office plant in Nobeoka, Miyazaki Prefecture, with operations scheduled to commence in
2027. TDK established a joint venture with Nippon Chemical Industrial in April 2026 called
TDK-NCI Advanced Materials (with equity stakes of 51% for TDK and 49% for Nippon
Chemical Industrial), with plans to accelerate development of the oxalate method barium
titanate.

Murata and SEMCO to benefit the most from growing
demand for high-value-added products for AI servers
We expect Murata and SEMCO, which supply small, high-capacity products stably and in
large volumes, to capture the greatest benefits from growing demand for small-sized,
high-capacity MLCCs used in AI servers and data centers. Murata has explained that AI/DC

MLCC sales accounted for 10-15% of its MLCC sales in F3/26 and are forecast to rise 100-
105% YoY in F3/27, accounting for 20-25% of MLCC sales. We estimate Murata's AI/DC
MLCC sales to rise roughly 120% YoY in F3/27 and about 100% YoY in F3/28, with AI/DC
MLCCs underpinning growth in the company's overall MLCC sales. Murata has stated that
it forecasts a 100-105% increase in AI/DC MLCC sales in F3/27, reflecting a roughly 40%
increase in volume and a roughly 50% rise in ASP, but we expect the increase in ASP to

exceed its forecast.
2025 MLCC market share was Murata 40.8%, Taiyo Yuden
11.3%
We estimate that global MLCC shipment value totaled $14.67bn in 2025 (up 10.3% YoY),
with shares of 40.8% for Murata, 22.5% for SEMCO, 11.3% for Taiyo Yuden, 6.9% for TDK,
and 5.4% for Yageo, and a combined 13.0% for all companies outside these top five. We
expect demand for high-value-added products for AI/DC to keep expanding from 2027,
with Murata and SEMCO continuing to gain market share.

What are the risks to Murata?
Risks for Murata include: (1) losing MLCC market share by failing to adequately supply
MLCCs for AI servers/DCs amid rapidly expanding demand. (2) Murata's products,
including MLCCs, are used not only in AI servers/DCs but across virtually all electronic
equipment, including automobiles, smartphones, and industrial machinery, making the
company sensitive to macroeconomic conditions and inventory cycles. (3) The company

has explained that a ¥1/$ appreciation of the yen would reduce operating profit by ¥4.5bn
per year, and the yen could appreciate beyond our assumed rate of ¥155/$, among other
factors.

Murata Manufacturing earnings forecasts
We expect Murata at 2Q results (end-Oct) to hike F3/27
guidance; continued earnings growth from F3/28
F3/27-28 and F3/27 2Q forecasts: We project F3/27 OP of ¥490.8bn (guidance ¥430bn,
FactSet consensus ¥453.9bn), up ¥208.9bn YoY and above F3/22's record of ¥424.1bn.
That is because we expect profit in the Components segment to rise ¥186.2bn to ¥501.3bn
and losses in the Device & Modules segment to shrink by ¥24.1bn from -¥26.5bn to -

¥2.4bn. We look for Components profit to rise due to a +31.6% YoY increase in MLCC
sales in dollar terms (+36.1% on yen basis), a continued increase after F3/26’s +13.8% rise
(+12.6% on yen basis), product mix improvement, and higher utilization rates absorbing
increased depreciation and other fixed cost growth. We expect Device & Modules losses
to shrink mainly due to the falling out of ¥43.8bn in Resonant goodwill impairment and
¥6bn from the disposal of the micro battery business. We project F3/28 OP of ¥702.6bn

(consensus ¥655.3bn). The projected ¥211.8bn YoY growth breaks down as Components +
¥170bn and Devices & Modules +¥39.6bn. We expect Components profit to rise thanks to
a 24.5% YoY increase in capacitor (MLCC) sales in dollars (+23.7% on yen basis) and
continued product mix improvement absorbing increased depreciation & other fixed cost
growth. We look for Devices & Modules profit to rise on factors including increased sales
of high-frequency modules, secondary batteries, and vertical power delivery modules.

We project 2Q OP of ¥125bn (consensus ¥122.9bn). The projected QoQ ¥26.6bn growth
breaks down as Components +¥12.5bn QoQ to ¥126bn, and Devices & Modules +¥13.9bn
QoQ to ¥1bn. For Components, we expect sales of MLCCs for AI/data centers to continue
to increase, the MLCC product mix to improve, and utilization rates to continue to rise. For
Devices & Modules, we expect sales for North American smartphones to increase
seasonally and reenter the black, but because North American smartphone production is

split between fall models and spring models, profit contributions are likely to be limited.

Murata’s share price risk/reward: Even though we raise our earnings estimates for

Murata, we lower our DCF-based price target from ¥12,500 to ¥11,000, mainly reflecting
an increase in our DCF model’s risk-free rate from 2.60% to 3.0%. Murata's share price has
fallen 41% year-to-date from its June 22 peak, and we see 53% upside over the next 12-18
months, maintain our OW rating, and designate the stock as our Top Pick in Japan's
electronic components industry. The reasons are as follows: (1) We forecast the dollar-
based shipment value of MLCCs, where Murata held the top global share of 40% in 2025,

to expand at a CAGR of 21% through 2031, with Murata continuing to gradually gain
market share. (2) Demand for MLCCs used in AI servers/DCs is likely to continue
expanding, and demand for small, high-capacity, high-value-added products, which Murata
has brought to market ahead of competitors, should grow substantially. (3) Outside of
MLCCs, we look for demand to expand for small, high-value-added products, including
secondary batteries for DC BBUs, power modules for vertical power delivery in AI servers,

and RF modules for smartphones, and then expand even further as edge AI devices

become widespread. (4) In addition to earnings growth, we expect that management’s
proactive approach to shareholder returns, including share buybacks, will raise capital
efficiency and lift ROE.
Bull case ¥14,000, 95% projected upside
In our bull case, we assume higher sales of MLCCs for AI servers/data centers than in our
base case, for component sales of 7% higher than our base case, for OP of ¥560.3bn in
F3/27, ¥794bn in F3/28, and ¥1.515tn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF

assumptions are the same as for our base case. Bull case fair value corresponds to 58.2x of
fully-diluted F3/27 EPS of ¥240.4, 41.1x fully-diluted F3/28 EPS of ¥340.7, and 21.1x fully-
diluted F3/31 EPS of ¥664.0.
Bear case ¥3,500
Murata products are used in all kinds of different electronic devices, and they are easily
affected by the macro economy and inventory cycle. In our bear case, we assume slower
global economic growth rates and lower production of high-end consumer goods like

high-end smartphones than in our base case for OP of ¥314.6bn in F3/27, ¥330bn in F3/28,
and ¥362.4bn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF assumptions are the same as in
our base case. Here we look for ROE to increase from 8.8% in F3/26 to 9.3% in F3/27 and
9.4% in F3/28, above capital cost of 7.5%. With profit below the market consensus, we
assume the share price falls to a P/B of 2.4x.

SEMCO earnings forecasts
Shawn Kim
AI-driven MLCC growth and pricing to drive sharp earnings
acceleration in FY27, with continued growth in FY28
FY26-28 forecasts: We project SEMCO OP of W2.34tn in FY26 (+156% YoY), W5.70tn in
FY27 and W8.36tn in FY28, implying further growth of 144% and 47%, respectively. We
expect the earnings acceleration to be driven mainly by the Components (MLCC) and
Package Solution businesses, while the Optics business remains a relatively modest

contributor. In FY27, our projected OP of W5.70tn breaks down as Components W4.56tn,
Package Solution W943bn, and Optics W201bn, versus W1.67tn, W510bn, and W165bn,
respectively, in FY26. In FY28, we forecast Components OP rising further to W6.55tn,
Package Solution to W1.58tn, and Optics to W230bn. As such, we expect SEMCO's consol.
OP margin to grow from 8.1% in FY25 to 15.7% in FY26, 24.5% in FY27, and 25.9% in FY28.
We maintain the stock as our top pick within our S. Korea Technology coverage.

For Components, we expect profit growth to accelerate as AI-server demand drives both
strong MLCC volume growth and a structurally richer product mix. High-end AI MLCC
supply remains tight, with lead times exceeding 20 weeks, supply-chain inventories lean
following two years of destocking, and book-to-bill at the three high-end suppliers
reaching its highest level since the pandemic. Importantly, contract pricing has increasingly
been institutionalized through long-term agreements, while the high-end AI MLCC market

remains concentrated, with Murata and SEMCO together holding around 85% share.
Capacity additions are also unlikely to close the supply-demand gap quickly, as most
meaningful new capacity is scheduled for 2027 or later and suppliers are generally
targeting only around 10-15% annual output growth. We thus expect tighter supply-
demand, higher pricing, and a shift toward high-capacitance, low-ESL AI products to drive
Components OP margin from 21.4% in FY26 to 30.9% in FY27 and 30.1% in FY28.

Bull case W3,000,000
Our bull case assumes ABF opportunities from major US CSPs drive stronger content
growth, with future ASIC content & volume growth continuing to outpace our base-case
expectations. Customer diversification toward non-mobile customers should also reduce
earnings volatility and improve profitability. Our bull case, equivalent to 15x FY27e P/B,
reflects what we see as an unprecedented super-cycle in the underlying AI-related MLCC

and ABF markets.
Bear case W1,350,000
In our bear case, we assume slower content growth in MLCCs and substrates due to
greater competition and China's localization, together with prolonged consumer weakness
weighing on the Optics business. However, we still expect increasing AI exposure to
structurally lift SEMCO's MLCC and ABF ASPs, product mix and margins, given the
difficulty commodity suppliers face in penetrating the premium market. Our bear-case

value corresponds to 7x FY27e P/B. Key downside risks include a significant pullback in

### Page 22

M
Japan Insight
22
Samsung's mobile flagship cycle, lower SEMCO market share, execution risk in penetrating
Chinese smartphone customers, and continued consumer-demand weakness.

Morgan Stanley MUFG 25
Yageo earnings forecasts
Howard Kao
Tightening MLCC Narrative is Unchanged
We forecast CY26 revenue of approximately NT$183B, up 38% y/y, driven primarily by
stronger AI-related demand, with a more modest contribution from consumer
applications. This demand strength should support broad-based utilization improvement
across Yageo’s passive component portfolio. Pricing is also turning more favorable, with
distributor price increases implemented in 2Q26 and Yageo raising prices for direct

customers in 3Q26, as previously highlighted. The combination of higher utilization and
firmer pricing should drive meaningful operating leverage. We forecast gross margin
expanding to 39.3%, up 310bps y/y, and operating margin reaching 27.4%, up 500bps y/y,
supporting EPS of approximately NT$19.79.
Looking into CY27, we expect supply/demand conditions in the mid- and low-end markets
to tighten further, sustaining pricing momentum and driving an additional improvement in

utilization. We therefore forecast revenue of approximately NT$252B, up 38% y/y.
Continued operating leverage should lift gross margin to 43.2%, an increase of 390bps y/y,
and operating margin to 33.4%, up 600bps y/y, resulting in EPS of approximately NT$32.11.
Why has the stock been so volatile?
Yageo benefited from a sharp increase in MLCC pricing in 2Q26, driven by a temporary
spike in spot-market demand. In our view, ODM/EMS customers accelerated inventory

replenishment amid concerns that the ramp in high-end AI-server MLCC demand,
particularly alongside Nvidia’s Vera Rubin platform, could constrain supply of mid- and
low-end MLCCs. Under normal market conditions, these customers typically carry only 3-4
weeks of inventory. However, supply concerns prompted a rapid increase in inventory
coverage to approximately 4-6 months.
During this restocking phase, ODM/EMS customers sourced MLCCs from both
manufacturers, including Yageo, and distributors. The resulting demand surge drove a

sharp increase in spot/distributor pricing and also contributed to the stock’s strong
performance. However, the pricing move was largely driven by a one-off inventory build.
Once customers had secured sufficient inventory, purchasing activity normalized and spot
pricing began to soften. This, together with weaker broader market sentiment, led to a
pullback of ~50% from the stock’s peak of approximately NT$1,140.
At the current share price, however, we believe the risk-reward has become attractive. The

underlying fundamentals, and, importantly, our view of the MLCC upcycle, remain intact.
Our analysis indicates substantial incremental demand for high-end, high-capacitance
MLCCs of 47µF and above, which will require a meaningful increase in dedicated
production capacity. We expect this to drive further capacity conversion toward high-end
MLCCs, with the resulting crowding-out effect becoming more visible from late CY26 into
early CY27. This should progressively tighten supply in the mid- and low-end MLCC

market and provide renewed support for pricing.

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
We lower our 12-month price target to NT$1,050 (from NT$1,465): This reflects our
lower earnings estimates for CY26-28. Our price target is our base case value, derived
from our residual income valuation model. Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware
companies within our coverage, we think this methodology provides the most accurate

value of the firm by taking into account the cost of equity.
Our key assumptions are:
• Cost of equity of 7.8%:
° Risk-free rate of 1.0% (10-year Taiwanese government bond yield);
° Equity risk premium of 6.8%;
° Beta of 1.1;

### Page 27

M
Japan Insight
Morgan Stanley MUFG 27
• Medium-term growth rate of 12% (down from 16%);
• Terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our price target implies 33x 2027e P/E and 24x 2028e P/E. These are above its five-year
historical range of 8x to 18x, which we view as justified considering its longer-term
transformation story to become a global total solutions provider, moving from passive
into active components. In addition, compared to global passive component peers, we

believe Yageo stock remains undervalued with higher ROE and margins but lower P/E vs.
Japanese peers (which trade on an average at 40x+ P/E).
Our bull and bear case scenario values decrease by magnitudes similar to the change
to our base case value, to NT$1,490 and NT$480, respectively.

Taiyo Yuden earnings forecasts
We expect expect record profit in F3/29, not F3/28; upgrade
the stock from UW to EW
Full-term F3/27 and F3/28 earnings forecasts, F3/27 2Q forecasts: We project F3/27 OP
of ¥45.1bn (guidance ¥45bn, FactSet consensus ¥42bn), up +¥25.1bn YoY. We expect sales
in dollars of core product capacitors (MLCCs) to increase +22.6% YoY (+27.4% on yen
basis), a continued increase following on FY3/26’s +10.4% rise (+8.5% on yen basis), and

higher utilization rates absorbing increased depreciation expense and other fixed cost
increases. We project F3/28 OP of ¥66.1bn (consensus ¥73.1bn), up +¥25bn YoY. We look
for sales in dollars of capacitors (MLCCs) to rise +17.3% YoY (+16.6% on a yen basis),
absorbing increased depreciation expense and other fixed cost increases. We expect OP to
rise ¥83.8bn in F3/29 (consensus ¥107.1bn), surpassing the historical high profit of
¥68.2bn in F3/22.

We project 2Q OP of ¥12.6bn (consensus ¥11bn), up +¥7.8bn from ¥4.8bn in 1Q. We look
for MLCC sales to rise +18.1% compared with 1Q and for the utilization rate to rise from
85% in 1Q to 95% in 2Q. With increased sales of inductors for North American
smartphones, there should be a major profit increase on 1Q.

Taiyo Yuden share price risk/reward: We upgrade Taiyo Yuden from UW to EW, as we
believe its share price, having fallen 56.0% from its July 1 peak, has limited downside risk

and offers 6% upside over the next 12-18 months. We lower our DCF-based price target
from ¥12,500 to ¥10,500. This mainly reflects an increase in the DCF model’s risk-free
rate from 2.60% to 3.0%. We rate the stock EW rather than OW because: (1) In its core
MLCC business, we estimate Taiyo Yuden's MLCC market share was 11.3% in 2025, down
from 12.7% in 2020, and we think this downtrend continued into 2026, with its market
share shrinking to 10.7% in 1Q and 9.9% in 2Q. (2) We believe Taiyo Yuden is lagging

behind Murata (No. 1 share) and SEMCO (No. 2) in small, high-capacity MLCCs for AI
server accelerator boards, where demand is rapidly expanding. (3) We expect consensus
estimates for F3/28 onward to be revised down after 2Q results.
Bull case ¥14,000, 49% projected upside
In our bull case, F3/27 sales of MLCCs for AI servers/data centers are 7% higher than in our
base case, for OP of ¥56.5bn in F3/27, ¥85.5bn in F3/28, and ¥176.3bn in F3/31. Our forex

assumptions and DCF assumptions match our base case. Bull case fair value corresponds
to 50.7x our fully-diluted F3/27 EPS estimate of ¥276.1, 33.6x our fully-diluted F3/28 EPS
estimate of ¥417.0, and 15.9x our full-diluted F3/31 EPS estimate of ¥879.1.
Bear case ¥4,500
Taiyo Yuden’s care products (MLCCs and inductors) are used in all kinds of electronic
devices, and are easily affected by the macro economy and inventory cycle. In our bear
case, we assume slower global economic growth rates and lower production of high-end

consumer goods like high-end smartphones than in our base case, for OP of ¥39.2bn in
F3/27, ¥56.1bn in F3/28, and ¥65.8bn in F3/31. Our forex assumptions and DCF
assumptions are the same as in our base case. Here we look for ROE of 7.3% in F3/27 and
10.1% in F3/28, improving to a level above capital cost of 7.9%. With profit below market
expectations, we assume the share price falls to a P/B of 1.8x.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Poya  (5904.TWO)
Positives Mostly Priced In; Downgrade to Neutral 
CITI'S TAKE 
We downgrade Poya to Neutral from Buy following its strong YTD share price 
performance. Although operating momentum remains positive in the near-
term (we expect a strong Sep SSSG and solid 3Q earnings), we believe the 
rally has already reflected much of the benefit from robust SSSG YTD and 
margin expansion. Looking ahead, tough comparisons from 4Q26E may 
make solid SSSG harder to sustain, limiting earnings upside and potential 
weighing on stock sentiment. Meanwhile, Cosmed’s expanding rollout of 
Olive Young brands adds to competition pressure for beauty spending. We 
trim our DCF-based TP to NT$70, implying 18x NTM P/E vs. 5-year mean of 
17x. We view current risk/reward as balanced. 
Strong performance leaves less room for further upside — Poya’s shares have 
rallied 73% YTD, outperforming the benchmark’s 57% gain and broadly flat 
performance for the TW retail sector. This reflects strong operating deliver, including 
6-7% YTD SSSG and solid margin expansion supported by op leverage and 
headcount optimization. While the company may continue to grow earnings through 
store expansion and productivity improvements, we believe much of this outlook is 
already priced in. Further share price appreciation may require additional earnings 
upside, which could become more difficult to deliver if SSSG moderates. 
Tougher comp from 4Q26E could temper SSSG momentum — Poya’s YTD SSSG 
has benefited from 4-5% ticket growth, partly reflecting price inflation, as well as 
traffic growth 1-2%. We expect the comp base to become more demanding from 
4Q26E onward, increasing the hurdle for keeping the current growth rate. Slower 
SSSG could reduce incremental op leverage and limit further margin expansion. 
Given its YTD rally, a shift to positive but slower growth could weigh on sentiment. 
Cosmed’s Olive Young rollout raises competition pressure — Cosmed introduced 
four Olive Young, Korea’s leading beauty retail channel, proprietary brands in late 
Dec 2025 and planned to expand dedicated sections to 120 stores by end-2026E 
( ~20% of Cosmed’s total store count), increasing local access to popular Korean 
beauty products. Per Cosmed, PSD at locations carrying OY brands increased by 
MSD y/y YTD vs. MSD y/y down across its overall network, suggesting the brands are 
gaining customer interest. We believe broader availability and product selection 
could intensity competition for customer visits and beauty spending, potentially 
slowing Poya’s traffic growth and increasing promotional pressure.  (continued)

Angela Hsu AC

Poya  (5904.TWO)

Poya (5904.TWO): Positives Mostly Priced In;
Downgrade to Neutral
Implications — We keep our earnings estimates largely unchanged for 2026-
2028E yet we trim our DCF-based TP to NT$70 from prior NT$74.5 mainly to factor
in a lower terminal growth of 4.5% (down from 5%). We also factor in its stock split
in Aug 2026. Our target price of NT$70 implies an 18x NTM forward P/E vs its 5-
year historical mean of 17x, which looks balanced to us.

We believe Poya’s store expansion and efficiency improvements may continue to
support earnings, but we see limited scope for further re-rating given the earnings
growth moderation (net profits +9% y/y in 2027E vs. +27% y/y in 2026E based on
our estimate) and its strong share-price gains to date. T ougher comparisons and
rising competition add uncertainty around the durability of SSSG momentum and
margin expansion.
We would prefer to see sustained traffic growth through the higher comp base,

clearer evidence of additional earnings upside or a more attractive entry point
before becoming more positive on the shares.

Bull/Bear: Poya  (5904.TWO)

### Page 6

Poya  (5904.TWO)
16 September 2026 Citi Research
6
Poya
Company description
Poya is the largest retailer of fast-moving consumer goods in Taiwan, focusing
on low-priced cosmetics, accessories, clothing and food products for females
aged 15-49. Poya had 470 stores in Taiwan at the end of 2025 and believes
that the market can accommodate up to 700-800 stores before reaching
saturation.
Investment strategy
We have a Neutral rating on Poya. We think YTD rally has baked in most

positives, including strong SSSG and solid margin expansion. While
fundamentals remain solid, tougher comparisons from 4Q26E could
moderate this year's 6-7% SSSG, lowering operating leverage and capping
further earnings upside. Meanwhile, Cosmed's expanding rollout of Olive
Young brands is intensifying competition for beauty spending. Although
Poya's SSSG has remained resilient, braoder distribution of these brands
could divert customer traffic and increase promotional pressure. We continue

to recognize Poya's execution in store expansion and ongoing efficiency
improvements. However, with these strengths largely reflected in the share
price and growth momentum facing a higher hurdle from 4Q26E onward, we
see a more balanced risk/reward.
Valuation
We derive our 12-month target price of NT$70 for Poya from a DCF valuation
method. Our assumptions include a terminal growth rate of 4.5% and WACC
of 10.1% (beta of 1.05; risk-free rate of 4%; risk premium of 7.0%; cost of equity

of 11.3%). Our target price implies a P/E of 18x on our NTM EPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: intense price competition among online retailers, slower store traffic,
margin dilution due to aggressive store expansion, and a soft macro
environment.
Key upside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: easing price competition among online retailers, stronger store

traffic, margin expansion thanks to strong cost control, and a stronger macro
environment.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

16 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
BizLink Holding
UBS T aiwan Summit 2026 key takeaways:
margins improve as HPC sales build
Positive outlook amid market volatilities
Bizlink's share price has been volatile amid gross margin weakness in Q126 and a
conservative read-through from Credo's earnings call on AEC products. Bizlink's
management remains positive about  its order momentum in HPC products, with data
cable shipments picking up, and power cable shipments  growing alongside rack

shipments and the higher content value of Vera Rubin. After the deal's completion, the
consolidation of Interplex should further expand the HPC sales contribution and drive
further margin expansion. Meanwhile, Bizlink has also identified opportunities in optical
connections through various partnerships. Earnings contributions from XFS are growing
robustly, though they remain a small proportion of the total. ALC and other CPO
revenue might not  contribute to earnings growth until H227, at the earliest.

Expect stronger HPC sales growth and margin expansion
Bizlink attributes the MoM sales decline in August to the summer holidays at its
European factories, which account for around one-third of capacity  and mainly supply
automation and industrial products. Management highlighted the historical patterns of
MoM improvements in September. Management remains positive about HPC and
capital equipment sales growth in the remainder of 2026. Data cables shipments have

recovered meaningfully since June. Power cables are growing rapidly with shipments of
Rubin servers, which have a much higher content value per rack than Blackwell. Further
upside could come from shipment of stand alone power side cars. The consolidation of
Interplex would serve as an additional growth driver after the deal is closed before end
of 2026. A higher sales mix from HPC could also drive gross margin expansion.
Engaging in various partnerships to capture optic interconnect opportunities

Management outlined several potential opportunities in optical connections: Credo's
ALC and other Micro-LED consortium products. These products provide mid-to-short
distance optical interconnection solutions. Credo plans to hold a demonstration in
October 2026, and Bizlink is the exclusive supplier. On CPO, Bizlink performs  fibre
assembly and supplies MPO connectors. It collaborates with FAU companies, including
Senko, and is working on solutions for shuffle boxes.

Valuation: Buy, price target at NT$3,617
We have a Buy rating with price target of NT$3,617, based on 30x 2027E PE.

Company Description
Founded in 1996, BizLink is headquartered in Fremont, California. BizLink provides
interconnect solutions, including connectors, harnesses and cables for a wide range of
industries, such as industrials, automotive, IT & data communications, and electrical

appliances. BizLink has 34 production facilities located in Asia, America and Europe.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a PE multiple.
The key company-specific risks are: 1) a global economic slowdown impacting end-demand;
2) a slower-than-expected change in the product mix leading to slower-than-expected
gross margin expansion; and 3) a rise in raw material prices.
The key industry-specific risks are: unexpected changes in the underlying global macro

environment, currency and interest rate movement and commodity price inflation.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 3

Taiwan Electronic Components & Equipment
17 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
Buy | TP NT$4,350.00 from NT$3,800.00 | Price NT$3,185.00 (17 Sep 26 13:30)
Sequential growth intact, ASIC carrying the upside — Our checks and mgmt.
comments indicate Vera Rubin platform has begun contributing in small volumes
from Sep, with a further ramp expected in 4Q26E. The ongoing ASIC project is now
shifting progressively from air cooling to liquid cooling, while another ASIC project

enters mass production in 4Q26E, together the main growth drivers as new
capacity comes online through the quarter. We now forecast 3Q26E/4Q26E
revenue growth at 18%/12% QoQ. GPM should also improve on a sequential basis,
supported by yield improvement and a rising contribution from server and liquid
cooling products.
ASIC contribution could exceed 50% of liquid cooling revenue in 2027 — With
cold plate demand continuing to run ahead of supply, we expect AVC to undertake

another round of capacity expansion in 2027 to meet rising ASIC demand,
consistent with mgmt. ’s view that ASIC contribution catches up with GPU in 2027.
We also note ongoing discussion around double-sided cold plate adoption, with
several platforms at the R&D stage, which we see as a source of upside
momentum. Rubin Ultra spec remains under development, and we do not expect
the potential TDP adjustments toward a lower level to affect content value, as cold

plate content is driven more by design than by chip TDP itself. On a higher liquid
cooling contribution, we forecast 2027E revenue to grow another 50% YoY, with
GPM to further increase to 34.9% (+2.2ppts YoY).
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 3%/13%/16% to
reflect the updated outlook, driven mainly by higher cold plate capacity and
shipment assumptions alongside a more constructive view on ASIC liquid cooling
demand. Our revised target price of NT$4,350 (from NT$3,800) remains based on

25x 2027E EPS. Given AVC’s position in the thermal sector and its valuation
following the recent share price pullback, we see a favorable risk-reward profile and
reiterate our Buy rating.

5
Auras Technology (3324.TWO)
Buy | TP NT$1,800.00 from NT$1,550.00 | Price NT$1,385.00 (17 Sep 26 15:00)
Liquid cooling ramp drives sequential acceleration into 4Q26 — Following Aug-
26 revenue, which pulled back from a high base in Jul-26, we believe Auras remains
on track to meet mgmt. ’s earlier guidance of at least 15% QoQ growth in 3Q26E.
Into 4Q26, shipments of Vera Rubin products, including inner/rack manifolds,

together with the start of mass production for liquid cooling version of ASIC
project, should allow Auras to deliver another solid sequential growth. We now
forecast 3Q26E/4Q26E revenue growth of 17%/16% QoQ, with GPM also on an
upward trend, supported by a higher liquid cooling revenue mix.
GPU & ASIC projects broaden the 2027 growth base — Our checks and mgmt.
comments  indicate Auras is still working through cold plate qualification for the
Vera Rubin platform and is now participating in next generation product

development. Given its operating scale, we believe even a modest share gain could
contribute meaningfully to its top line, and we expect the earliest potential
contribution from 1Q27E. Auras should also benefit from rising ASIC liquid cooling
demand, with existing ASIC project shipments to expand above 4Q26E levels and
several further projects now under discussion. We forecast 2027E revenue growth
of 35% YoY, with liquid cooling mix rising from 61% in 2026E to 70% in 2027E and

GPM reaching 31.3% (+1.2ppts YoY).
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 2%/14%/17% to
reflect the updated outlook, driven mainly by higher cold plate shipment
assumptions alongside a more constructive view on ASIC liquid cooling demand.
Our revised target price of NT$1,800 (from NT$1,550) remains based on 20x 2027E
EPS. Given Auras’ structural growth in the server business, widening liquid cooling
content across both GPU and ASIC platforms, and an optimizing global

manufacturing footprint in Thailand, we view the current valuation as attractive
and reiterate our Buy rating.

Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible position as
Blackwell and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider
share of the thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU
opportunities, providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$4,350 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.
Auras Technology
Company description
Auras Technology was founded in 1998 and focused on the design and

manufacturing of thermal modules for NB/PCs. It quickly became one of
Taiwan’s top two NB cooling component suppliers. Early products included
heatsinks, fins, heat pipes, and fans for NBs, PCs, and smartphones. As
device form factor evolved, the company invested in new technologies such
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 12

Taiwan Electronic Components & Equipment
17 September 2026 Citi Research
12
as ultra-thin vapor chamber in the 2000s to address the thermal challenges
of increasingly compact consumer electronics. During the 2010s, Auras
expanded into graphic card and server cooling and was an early adopter of
liquid cooling technology. By 2012, the company had begun developing liquid
cooling component and was among the first Taiwanese firms to venture into
AI server liquid cooling.

Investment strategy
We rate Auras a Buy. Auras is emerging as a key liquid-cooling supplier as
hyperscalers expand deployment. The company has secured qualifications for
cold plates and manifolds, opening access to a wider slate of Nvidia and CSP
projects. Thailand-based production offers non-China capacity at a scale
aligned with hyperscaler sourcing plans, allowing Auras to compete more
effectively for next-generation products.
Valuation
We value Auras using a PE-based approach, reflecting its accelerating

earnings trajectory and rising share in liquid cooling market of AI servers. Our
TP of NT$1,800 is based on 20x 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified
given the company’s expanding server revenue base, deeper engagement
with CSP customers, and broader product portfolio across cold plates, CDMs,
and CDUs.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model, but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported in our view by other qualitative factors such

as rapid RVL progress and Thailand expansion, and so a High Risk rating has
not been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target
price include: 1) slower adoption of liquid cooling; 2) heightened price
competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components;
and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
CCL –  Doosan announces
capacity expansion in Korea and
China
What’s new? According to Business Korea, Doosan (000150.KS, not covered)
announced a KRW970B CCL capacity expansion on Sep 17:
• KRW420B in Korea to add optical module CCL lines, and
• KRW550B in China to build a new plant for AI accelerator and network
switch CCL.
Spending is phased through 2028, with the new lines coming online sequentially

from 2H28.
Our thoughts:  Our checks suggest this investment could add ~2,000K sheets per
month of CCL capacity at Doosan, equivalent to ~2% of global CCL capacity in 2028,
on our estimates. We view the investment as further evidence of robust demand for
high-end CCL and do not believe it undermines our thesis of continued high-end CCL
scarcity (see our CCL initiation report). As such, we see a positive read-across for
EMC, TUC, and Shengyi. We remain OW on EMC and TUC, while maintaining our EW

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Wiwynn (6669.TW): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion; 
Aug strong beat; TP up to NT$3,777; Buy
21 September 2026 | 6:21AM HKT
Wiwynn delivered strong Aug revenues, which were 27% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general servers demand from US leading CSPs, and its major 
customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up. We expect sequential revenues growth 
to continue through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +46% / 
+22% QoQ, driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across 
air-cooled and liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on 
agentic AI, (3) new GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 
across NVIDIA and AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI 
servers. Maintain Buy with TP raised to NT$3,777 (21.5x 2027E P/E vs. +32% EPS YoY 
in 2027-28E).

Ongoing capacity expansion: As we highlighted in Aug (report link), Wiwynn 
announced a 2H26 capex budget of US$942m, implying 2026 capex could be up to 
NT$40bn (US$1.3bn). On Sep 16, the company announced equipment procurement 
of US$16m (NT$512m), reflecting the strong AI demand and the company’s 
commitments on capacity expansion. We estimate the company’s capex at NT$44bn 
in 2026E, and to stay at a similar high level through 2028E, supporting its production 
sites diversification across the US, Mexico, and Malaysia, AI servers expansion from 
ASIC to GPU-powered, and products expansion across different ASICs and GPUs, 
along with further customers penetration. 
Earnings revision: We factor in August revenues, and change our net income by +7% 
/ +5% / +3% in 2026-28E. The change is mainly on revenues: we raise 2026-28E 
revenues by 8% / 7% / 5%, reflecting the strong beat of Aug revenues driven by

growing AI demand, which is also driving its general servers growth. Our EPS are reduced 
as we update the share count, reflecting the company’s stock split that started from 
Sept 2026. The mathematical effect on our EPS is around 66%.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our 2027E 
target P/E increases slightly to 21.5x (vs. 20x previously), which is still derived from the 
peers’ updated correlation of forward trading P/E and EPS growth. Accordingly, our 12M 
TP increases by 13% to NT$3,777.00 (vs. NT$3,352.55 previously). Our target P/E of 
21.5x is above the company’s historical avg.+1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on Wiwynn’s leading position in ASIC AI server ODM along with product 
expansion (from ASIC to GPU AI servers and general server) and customers expansion. 
Maintain Buy.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$3,777. Our target price is 
based on 21.5x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

本次報告：論壇

欣興 (3037 TT) 
ABF 漲幅優於預期，獲利再度上修

永豐觀點 
3Q26 欣興 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，中線T-glass 玻纖布於 3Q27 前
供給吃緊，長線 ABF 供不應求至 2030 年，載板報價可望逐季推升 5-15%，獲利
持續高速成長，近期股價下跌反應檢調搜索利空，評價已修正至合理區間。 
 投資評價與建議 
欣興受惠 ABF 帶動產品組合轉佳，長線而言 ABF 於 2030 年前供給吃緊，載板報
價漲勢由成本推升改為需求拉動，3Q26 漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點，且本波循環
公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 訂單，客戶遍及 GPU、ASIC 及 CPU 訂單，技術能
力僅次於龍頭廠 Ibiden， 研究部再度上調2026-2028 年稅後 EPS 至 23.57、42.71
及 65.08 元，投資建議買進，考量檢調風波對評價承壓，目標價維持 1575 元
(24X2028 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
欣興企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2023 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

### Page 2

2

欣興為國內 ABF 龍頭廠商：欣興為國內專業 IC 載板製造商，全球市佔率 18%僅
次於 Ibiden 以及 Samsung，2Q26 產品組合為 ABF(52%)、BT(9%)、HDI(27%)、
PCB(9%)、FPC(2%)及其它(1%)，其中 IC 載板毛利率優於公司平均水準。下游客
戶包含 Nokia、APPLE、Nvidia 以及 HTC，主要競爭對手為 Ibiden、Samsung、
Shinko、南電以及景碩。 
3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%， 暗示ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 ：欣興 8 月營收來到 177.46
億元(+9.2%MoM，+56.3%YoY)，除延續自 3 月以來月月創高紀錄外值得留意的
是月增及年增幅度亦同步擴大，整體 3Q26 營收達成率 69.6%亦優於原預期，暗
示 ABF 報價漲幅優於預期 10 個百分點以上，研究部上調3Q26 營收至 525.83 億
元(+22.6%QoQ，+54.7%YoY)， 毛利率25.8%， 稅後淨利93.36 億元(-28.8%QoQ，
+325.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 5.68 元(原估 5.26 元)。 
CPU 生命週期展開使 ABF 需求更具延續性：過去 ABF 需求主要聚焦 AI Server 需
求，研究部原預估 2024-2025 年 ABF 供過於求 2%，2026 年亦難見反轉。受惠
AI Server 於 GPU 及 ASIC 需求帶動，另一方面 general server 回溫有望使 ABF
需求由高階產品延續至中階產品，Ibiden 預估相關高階 ABF 需求高速成長，加計
Production load 換算 2028 年面積需求較 2024 年增加 10.7 倍 ， 相較AI/ASIC 產
能於 2028 年擴充 1.8 倍供給仍低於需求。 
檢調搜索風波影響營運有限：日前欣興合江廠遭檢調搜索，股價亦應聲下跌，研究
部認為欣興合江廠位於中壢，主要生產 PCB 產品 2Q26 營收占比 9%，雖然後續
可能因產地標示不明影響 PCB 客戶關係 ， 惟與目前主力營運ABF 載板以及 HDI 並
無關聯。ABF 在 AI Server 需求推動下 2030 年以前供不應求，目前高階 GPU、
ASIC 產品僅 Ibiden(4062 JP)及欣興(3037 TT)具備相關供應能力，自研 CPU 則有
景碩(3189 TT) 緊追在後，考量產品認證需時 6-8 個月、關鍵壓合機台 lead time
亦 1-1.5 年，短期轉單不易。 
ABF 報價優於預期，上調 2026-2028 年營運：欣興 2026 年資本支出 537 億元，
其中 85%用於 ABF 除搭配客戶陸續啟動光復及楊梅 2 廠擴產，楊梅 3 廠可望於
4Q26-1Q27 動土，以土建時程 1.5-2 年推算 2029 年可望加入營運行列，未來 2
年資本支出亦可望維持 400 億以上水準。研究部考量 ABF 整體供給吃緊態勢延續
以及公司通吃 CoWoS 及 EMIB-T 產品，先進封裝客戶群布局廣泛，研究部上調

## Document 137: 3324雙鴻 國票

Source PDF: all report pdf/3324雙鴻 國票.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-09
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

請參閱末頁之聲明

2026.09.09 
徐文華 C00215@ibfs.com.tw 
3324.TT 雙鴻 
 水冷板重回 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 供應鏈

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，主要生產散熱器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，
應用於筆記型電腦 、 桌上型電腦 、 伺服器 、 投影機等領域 ， 主要終端客戶為Apple(AAPL 
US)、Dell(DELL US)、Samsung(005930 KS)、HP(HPQ US)、 宏碁(2353)、 華碩(2357)
等。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,620 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，1,260 
【大盤指數/股價】47106 / 1340

➢ 2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫  
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%， 營收月減是受客
戶拉貨排程影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致。雙鴻 1H26 伺服器營收占比
已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替期，整體
2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持
全年營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在
4Q26~1Q27 明顯推升雙鴻業績 ， 且ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入 ，
此部分效益可挹注到 2027 年；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300
為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin cold plate， 已回到NVIDIA 推薦名單 ，
預期 4Q26 小量出貨；3)其他新品方面，DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26
小量出貨。CPU 均熱片 2026 年營收占比為個位數，要到 2027~2028
年效益才會較明顯。 
➢ 2027 年泰國持續擴產 
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國，泰國廠為
近年擴產重點 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、
manifold、Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/
月、manifold 3k/ 月；2)泰國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產
cold plate 、manifold，並預留電源水冷產能，若各產品線產能順利拉
升 ， 未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍 ；3)泰國三期規劃在 4Q26 動工 、2H27
投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工。 
➢ 建議調升至買進，目標價 1,620 元  
雙鴻 2H26 營運展望正向，AI 伺服器的強勢增長，能彌補消費性電子市
況低迷帶來的不利影響。 3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量，並且
cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量
出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE
區間為 8X~36X， 考量新品可逐步帶動2H26~1H27 之業績 ， 且目前PE
評價偏低，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,620 元(2027F PE 20X)。

3324 雙鴻 加權指數

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫 
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%，營收月減是受客戶拉貨排程
影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致(7 月營收包含 6 月客戶盤點遞延拉貨的 3 億元)。雙鴻
1H26 伺服器營收占比已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替
期，整體 2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持全年
營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世 ，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在 4Q26~1Q27 明
顯推升雙鴻業績，且 ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入，此部分效益可挹注到 2027
年。雙鴻估計 2026 年底 ASIC 客戶將占單月營收達 40%，2027 年 ASIC 營收占比將
擴增至 50%；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300 為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin 
cold plate ，已回到 NVIDIA 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量出貨，如此一來公司能提供
涵蓋 cold plate 、Rack Manifold 、Inner Manifold 的完整方案；3)其他新品方面，
DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26 小量出貨。CPU 均熱片(Heat Spreader)2026 年營收占
比為個位數，要到 2027 年~2028 年效益才會較明顯。 
投研部預期，在新舊平台完成交接，新品順利放量的情境下，雙鴻伺服器營收占比將
上升至 80%以上，受惠新品效應與水冷的導入，可帶動毛利率回升，投研部預估雙鴻
3Q26 營收 101.9 億元，QoQ+17.2%，YoY+71.1%，毛利率 29.8%，EPS 達 15.40
元。 
圖 1：雙鴻產品組合 1Q25~2Q26

資料來源：雙鴻 
2027 年泰國持續擴產   
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國， 泰國廠為近年擴產重點 。
各廠區規劃如下 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、manifold、
Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/月、manifold 3k/月；2)泰
國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產 cold plate、manifold，並預留電源水冷
產能，若各產品線產能順利拉升，未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍； 3)泰國三期規劃在
4Q26 動工、2H27 投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工；5)中國廠區負責生產筆電、
高階顯示卡 、 伺服器散熱相關產品 ， 整體上 ， 以氣冷模組較多 ， 也有少量的cold plate、
manifold；6)台灣主力生產 CDU (Cooling Distribution Unit)，並負責高階與高精度
液冷產品的研發和樣品試產。

個股報告 
建議調升至買進，目標價 1,620 元 
雙鴻 2H26 營運展望正向，AI 伺服器的強勢增長，能彌補消費性電子市況低迷帶來的
不利影響 。3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量 ， 並且cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA 
Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有
利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE 區間為 8X~36X，考量新品可逐步帶動 2H26~1H27 之業
績，且目前 PE 評價偏低，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,620 元(2027F PE 20X)。

為 38.72% 與 105.43% ，在產能滿載帶動，預估日成 KY 3Q26 營
收金額 9.00 億元， YoY+44.22% ，QoQ+39.31% ，預估營業利益
0.96 億元， YoY+185.3% ，QoQ+198.63% ，預估稅後盈餘 0.65
億元， YoY+199.85% ，QoQ+198.93% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.33 元。

二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 0.87 元 
進入 4Q26 傳統淡季，預估日成 KY 4Q26 營收金額 7.89 億元，
YoY+51.27% ， QoQ-12.33% ，預估營業利益 0.66 億元，
YoY+169.35% ， QoQ-31.24% ，預估稅後盈餘 0.42 億元，
YoY+238.86% ，QoQ-34.85% ，預估稅後 EPS 0.87 元。

三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.00 元 
日成 KY 2026 年在主要客戶受到銀價上漲，新產品規劃以鉑金產
品為大宗 ， 其中日成KY 電鍍產能在經濟規模與品質良率受惠客戶
新產品訂單較大份額，將帶動營收成長；在獲利方面，在產品組
合優化與調整代工費帶動毛利率提升，進而帶動獲利成長。宏遠
預估日成 KY 2026 年營收金額 29.50 億元， YoY+38.62% ，預估
稅後盈餘 1.96 億元，由虧轉盈，預估稅後 EPS 4.00 元。

四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 4.50 元 
日成 KY 2027 年在主要客戶新產品推出，刺激下游終端需求回
溫，進而推升主要客戶訂單維持高檔水準，其餘客戶方面，受惠
滲透率提升與拓展新地區業務，綜合上述，日成 2027 年營運維持
成長態勢。預估日成 KY 2027 年營收金額 32.00 億元，
YoY+8.48% ，預估稅後盈餘 2.20 億元， YoY+12.51% ，預估稅後
EPS 4.50 元。

2

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
日成控股成立於 1989 年，資本額 4.90 億元，並於 2017 年掛牌
上市，日成控股為流行珠寶及飾品設計製造出口企業，初期公司
名為日成有限公司。 1991 年公司更名為日成珠寶製造股份有限公
司，經過數年的經營，生產規模擴大，生產工藝及設計能力深受
國際客戶喜愛，並在當地珠寶及飾品設計製造產業享負盛名。有
鑑於公司成長及管理需求，積極推動專業管理方式，於 2003 獲得  
ISO 9001 認證。另，於 2004 年導入  ERP 系統，以因應公司未
來業務需求，快速成長趨勢，資源整合，以達有效管理及運用。
科技日新月異，日成公司為滿足客戶要求，不斷增強研發能力，
於 2007 年大量投入具有較強的設計能力及使用最先進的技術 ， 已
拓展生產能方面機器，如沖壓鑄造、金屬多樣鑄臺，並軟體以及
使用 3D 設計技術軟件等，設計出時尚與滿足客戶需求之產品。

圖一、日成控股身處產業中游地位

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

二. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
日成 -KY 主要營業項目為珠寶首飾設計、製造與銷售，產品包含
吊墜、戒指、耳環、手環、手鍊、項鍊與服飾配件等，主要生產
基地位於泰國曼谷近郊的龍仔厝，基地面積 6.59 萬平方米，員工
人數在 3100 人，滿載月產值在 3~3.5 億元。日成主要客戶多為國
際精品大廠，銷售區域遍布歐美亞三大洲，且日成控股泰國工廠
規模符合國際品牌代工條件，標準化生產製程具效率，且具有
ODM 設計開發能力。此外，公司也是泰國少數同時具有大規模產
能及電鍍產線的 ODM 代工廠。

圖二、珠寶產業分佈

資料來源：公司、宏遠投顧整理

觀察全球主要珠寶品牌商受到白銀價格大幅上漲侵蝕利潤與下游
終端客戶需求，因此將加速鉑金  (Platinum) 系列的轉型與推廣，
以多樣化產品線應對成本波動。為降低對白銀的依賴，計畫將部
分暢銷產品從銀製改為鍍鉑金設計，同時將產品線中銀製品的比
例從約 60% 降至 20% ，並希望在 2028 年前完成此轉型。由於鉑
金價格遠高於白銀，因此會在合金基底上鍍鉑金，既可減少鉑金
使用量，也有助控制成本，最終售價與銀製品相同，將有助廠商
降低銀價上漲侵蝕利潤的情況。

日成 KY 01~08/2026 營收金額 18.47 億元， YoY+39.00% 。日成
2026 年營運動能主要包括： 1，全球珠寶產業在 2022~2023 年經
歷後疫情時代庫存調整， 2024 年己有止穩並小幅回升情況， 2025
年在產業景氣持續回升，加上新材料實驗室鑽石價格也跌至甜蜜
點，品牌商將積極開發實驗室鑽石珠寶，有助整體產業 2026 年持
續回溫。 2，觀察第一大客戶 2021~2025 年營收占比，除 2023 年
占比 29% 較低外，其餘年度營收占比落在 43~52% ，在珠寶市場
己開始轉型鉑金產品，在電鍍加工的效率與良率將成為製程關
鍵，日成在電鍍品質與產能為泰國優秀廠商，預期在第一大客戶
在新產品推出帶動下， 2026 年營收將成長 35~40% 。3，在澳洲
區新客戶 (經營 3 年多 )與北美新客戶 (經營兩年多 )在多年來的深耕
經營與合作默契培養，預期 2026 年新客戶營收占比將有明顯提
升。預估日成 KY 2026 年營收金額 29.50 億元， YoY+38.62% 。

預期 2027 年，在原有客戶產品改款帶動下游需求熱絡，預期第一
大客戶 2027 年營收將小幅成長 5~10% ，其餘客戶方面，受惠滲
透率提升與新地區業務開拓帶動下 ， 預期2027 年營收成長幅度將
落在 15~20% 。綜合上述，預估日成 KY 2027 年營收金額 32.0 億
元， YoY+8.48% 。

1 
6173 信昌電(PDC)

報告日期： 2026/09/11 研究員：洪瑞陽  (Ryan Hung) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 13.76 億元， EPS 1.79 元 
2Q26 合併營收 13.76 億元 ，QoQ +35.3% 、YoY +23.9% ，隨 AI Server 
Power 需求加速拉貨，單季營運明顯轉強。毛利率 28.8% ，QoQ 
+0.4ppts 、 YoY +5.2ppts ；營業利益率 22.3% ，較去年同期提升
5.7ppts ，帶動營業利益 3.06 億元、 YoY +66.3% ，獲利增幅顯著高
於營收 。 稅後淨利3.06 億元 、YoY +671% ，EPS 1.79 元；累 計1H26
營收 23.92 億元、 YoY +16% ，EPS 2.98 元。獲利結構改善主要來
自產品組合由去年同期車用轉向 AI 大尺寸、高功率及高壓
MLCC ，AI 營收占比由 1Q26 的 6.5% 提升至 2Q26 的 9.2% ，帶動
毛利率與營益率同步走升。 8 月單月營收達  5.52 億元，月增
2.69% ，年增 54.27% ，寫下自 2021 年 7 月以來的單月新高紀錄。

二、 AI Power 帶動大尺寸 MLCC(Mega Cap 、NP0、高
壓 X7/X8 同步放量 ) 
成長由 Mega Cap 、NP0 及高溫高壓 X7/X8 等大尺寸特殊 MLCC
構成，主要切入 AI Server 的 PSU 、BBU 、CBU 等 Power 端， AI
營收占比由 1Q26 的 6.5%、2Q 的 9.2%，預估 3Q、4Q 進一步提升
至 12% 、16% ，為產品組合及毛利率改善核心。  
NP0 主要用於 LLC 迴路，目前約占公司營收 10% 且已滿載； Mega 
Cap 則於 2H26 開始大量放量 ， 產品以既有MLCC 進行後加工及堆
疊，一般產品 ASP 若為 1，Mega Cap 約可達 7~8 倍，隨堆疊數增
加單價可再提高，因此不僅增加出貨量，亦可明顯提高既有產能
產值。 X7/X8 則受惠 AI Power 高溫、高壓及高可靠度要求同步增
加，公司大尺寸產品主要供應兩大台系電源廠。供給端目前已轉
為產能受限，公司 2Q 後產線接近滿載、訂單能見度約 12 個月，
2026 年楊梅一廠整體 MLCC 產能較去年增加約 30% ；另一方面，
2H26 既有產品平均調漲約 30% ，價格效益逐步反映，新 AI 產品
則以高 ASP 產品放量為主。展望 2027 年，楊梅二廠預計 2Q27 完
工、2H27 量產，整體 MLCC 產能較現有水位再增加接近一倍，其
中 NP0 產能較既有增加約 60 ～ 70% ；六甲粉末廠產能增加約
50% ，並由 250nm 進一步往 200nm 高階粉材發展，配合中高壓及
大尺寸高階 MLCC 擴產，成長強勁。

三、預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 8.31/15.15 元 
2H26 受惠一廠新增產能、既有產品漲價及 Mega Cap 開始放量，
投資評等：買進 
目標價位：378 元 
      25X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：295 元 
     19.4X 2027 EPS 
     2026/09/10 收盤價

預估 3Q26 ／4Q26 營收 16.86 ／20.93 億元， AI 營收占比由 2Q 的
9.2% 提高至 12% ／16%；隨 NP0、Mega Cap 及 X7/X8 等高毛利特
殊品占比提升，預估毛利率由 2Q26 的 28.8% 進一步升至 3Q／4Q
的 31.58% ／34.07%。預 估2026 年營收 61.72 億元（YoY +52.4% ）、
毛利率 31.28% 、營業利益率約 24.2% ，稅後 EPS 8.31 元。

2027 年除 Mega Cap 及 NP0 完整貢獻全年外， 2H27 楊梅二廠新增
接近一倍 MLCC 產能開始加入，其中 NP0 較既有產能增加 60～
70% ，六甲粉末新廠亦同步開出，支撐高階產品持續放量。預估
AI 營收占比全年提升至 22% ，全年營收 103.56 億元（ YoY 
+67.8% ）；高 ASP 特殊品占比進一步提高，惟 2H 新廠折舊開始
反映於製造成本 ， 預估全年毛利率35.17% 、 營業利益率約28.0% ，
稅後 EPS 15.15 元。

四、投資建議：買進，目標價 378 元 
信昌電由過去消費性 、 網通及車用產品 ， 逐步轉向AI Server Power
所需的大尺寸、高壓、高功率特殊 MLCC ，現階段需求已由景氣
復甦轉為 AI 帶動的結構性成長。短期受惠既有產品漲價、一廠擴
產及 Mega Cap 放量 ， 中期則由楊梅二廠與六甲粉末廠開出支撐量
能，且 Mega Cap 、NP0 及 X7/X8 等特殊品 ASP 與毛利率均高於
既有產品，使營收成長同時具備量增及產品組合升級效果，且為
台廠唯二有能力供應全球大尺寸高壓高容 MLCC 廠商，以 27 年
EPS 15.15 元計算，給予 25 倍本益比，目標價 378 元，建議買進。

貳、公 司 概 況 
一、公司簡介  
信昌電成立於 1990 年，主要從事 MLCC 、貼片電阻及介電陶瓷粉
末的研發與製造，是國內是台灣唯一具備介電陶瓷粉末自製能力
的公司。公司台灣主要生產據點包括桃園廠及楊梅廠，桃園廠主
要生產陶瓷粉末及貼片電阻，楊梅廠則以 MLCC 為主；另楊梅二
廠及六甲粉末新廠預計 2027 年下半年量產。信昌電長期聚焦高功
率 、 高電壓 、 大尺寸及高可靠度被動元件 ， 產品包括MLCC、Chip-R
及介電陶瓷粉末。其中 2026 年上半年營收以 MLCC 為主，占約
68% ，其次為電阻約 16% 及粉末約 12% ，主力仍集中於 MLCC 。
公司應用市場則逐步由過去消費性電子轉向工業、 AI Server 
Power 、車用及特殊應用。從終端應用來看，公司工業應用占比持
續提升， 2026 年 7 月已達 53% ，主要受 AI 電源及其他高功率工
業需求帶動； AI 應用營收占比則由 1Q26 的 6.5% 提升至 2Q26 的
9.2% ，公司預期 4Q26 可進一步提升至 16% 。 
一、信昌電主要產品示意圖

二、 AI Power 帶動需求， Mega Cap 、NP0 開始放量  
信昌電 AI 產品主要集中在 Power 端，並非主機板上的小尺寸
MLCC ，而是 PSU 、BBU 等電源系統使用的大尺寸、高壓及高可
靠度產品。過去手機、 NB 等消費性產品對大尺寸元件需求低，大
尺寸 MLCC 在整體市場用量占比不高； 2025 年大於 1206 尺寸產
品用量占整體 MLCC 僅約 1.49% ，供應商因此長期較少。隨 AI 伺
服器功率快速提升，電源端電壓、功率及可靠度要求同步提高，
大尺寸 MLCC 需求開始明顯增加，公司長期深耕的產品規格因而
由過去利基市場轉為 AI Power 的重要元件。

AI 產品主要以 Mega Cap 及高壓低損耗 NP0 兩項特殊品為主 。NP0
具低損耗、耐高溫及穩定性，主要使用於 Power 的 LLC 迴路，目
前約占公司營收 10% ；隨功率提升， LLC 需要以多顆 NP0 堆疊達
到所需容量，公司產品規格亦由 630V ～1000V 持續往 1250V 發
展。高階 NP0 單機用量由 A100 世代約 800 ～1,000 顆，H100 增加
至 1,800 ～2,000 顆，至 GB300 已提升至約 3,000 ～5,000 顆，後續
800V HVDC 架構仍將維持大量高壓元件需求。

Mega Cap 則為堆疊式 MLCC ，主要訴求高可靠度、散熱及降低斷
裂風險，可應用於 AI Server Power 、BBU 及 AI Mini Station 。過
去 Mega Cap 因單價高、使用量有限，但 AI 設備整機價值高，電
源端對元件失效容忍度低，可靠度的重要性高於單純元件成本，
因此開始增加使用。 Mega Cap 單價約為一般單體 MLCC 的 7～8

公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

倍以上，依堆疊數量增加後 ASP 還可進一步提高；製程上則是在
既有 MLCC 基礎上進行後加工及堆疊，不需要同比例增加前段產
能，因此特殊品占比提高可明顯增加單位產能產值及毛利。

以 AI Server 次系統來看，大尺寸高壓 MLCC 主要使用於 PSU 、
BBU 、主板及 Switch ，其中 PSU 與 BBU 用量最高；估算 GB300
／ Vera Rubin 等世代單一機櫃電源與備援系統的大尺寸高壓
MLCC 用量可達上萬顆。隨 GB300 及 Vera Rubin 於 2H26 加速拉
貨，公司 AI 營收占比已由 1Q26 的 6.5% 提升至 2Q26 的 9.2%，預
估 Q3、Q4 分別提升至 12% 、16% ；其中 Mega Cap 與客戶已合作
驗證約 2～3 年，真正放量集中於 2H26 ，因此下半年 AI 營收成長
斜率將明顯高於上半年。本報告預估 27 年全年 AI 營收占比約
22% ，4Q27 約 26% 。

三、特殊品供給吃緊，舊品漲價、新品放量推升毛利  
大尺寸 MLCC 過去占整體 MLCC 用量僅約 1.49% ，市場規模相對
小，供應商長期投入有限；大尺寸產品單價遠高於一般 MLCC ，
價值占總體 MLCC 約 20～30%。目前台灣高功率大尺寸 MLCC 主
要供應商為信昌電及 H 公司，公司主要供應台系兩家電源大廠。

公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

5 
日系廠商雖具備相關技術，但產能主要投入小尺寸高容產品，大
尺寸 X7／X8 及 NP0 投入相對有限；陸系廠商目前在高壓 Power
特殊品的供應能力亦較有限，信昌電長期投入大尺寸 MLCC ，且
有地理優勢配合台系客戶，因此本輪新需求主要集中在高容、高
壓特殊品，而非 2018 年一般品 MLCC 全面缺貨的情況，基本面正
向許多。  
需求自 2Q26 開始明顯轉強，公司目前訂單遠超現有產能，訂單能
見度約 12 個月，交期已拉長至約 30 週，NP0 等 AI 特殊品則優先
安排生產。供給吃緊下，公司 2H26 針對既有產品及舊客戶進行價
格調整，平均漲幅約 30% ，部分規格依供需狀況有所差異； AI 等
新產品則是在 Design-in 時即依特殊規格訂價，目前主要成長來自
需求放量而非漲價，後續價格待新年度合約重談時再進行調整。
因此下半年營運有兩項不同動能：既有產品受惠漲價提高 ASP ，
AI 端則由 Mega Cap 、NP0 等原本單價與毛利較高的特殊品快速放
量，推升整體產品組合。

此產品組合變化已反映在獲利能力。 2024 年公司成長仍主要來自
電動車，至 4Q25 開始轉向 AI Server Power ；2Q26 客戶確認需求
後加速拉貨，單季營收 QoQ 成長約 30% ，毛利率提升至 28.8% ，
相較去年同期增加 5.2 個百分點 。 公司表示毛利率改善主要來自產
品組合，而非漲價，因今年出貨已由去年同期車用產品轉向 AI 大
尺寸、高功率及高壓特殊品；隨 2H26 Mega Cap 放量、 AI 占比提
高及既有產品漲價效益逐步反映。

三、訂單遠超於產能， 2H27 新廠產能滿載貢獻獲利  
信昌電目前 MLCC 及材料產能稼動率滿載， AI 及大尺寸產品訂單
已超過現有供應能力，因此短期營收成長主要受產能限制。楊梅
一廠持續進行去瓶頸及產能擴充， 2026 年 MLCC 產能較去年增加
約 30% ；由於 Mega Cap 屬後加工產品，公司亦可透過提高 Mega 
Cap 產品占比，在前段產能有限下提高單位產能產值。

下一階段擴產則集中於楊梅二廠及六甲粉末新廠。楊梅二廠主要
生產高壓、低損耗及高溫穩定型 MLCC ，預計 2Q27 完工、 2H27
開始量產，開出後 NP0 相關產能較目前可再增加約 60～70% ；六
甲新廠則建置新一代自動化陶瓷粉末產線，除提高生產效率及粉
末產能約 50% ，亦將粉末粒徑由既有 250nm 進一步往 200nm 發
展，以支援更高階 MLCC 產品。兩座新廠皆預計 2027H2 量產，
將成為目前產能吃緊後的下一階段成長動能。

除 AI 外，公司亦逐步布局機器人、自駕車及低軌衛星等高功率應
用。機器人主要切入關節馬達、無框力矩馬達及控制板，隨系統

由過去 12V 逐步提升至 48V 甚至 72V，對100 ～150V 大尺寸 MLCC

## Document 175: 8103瀚荃 國票

Source PDF: all report pdf/8103瀚荃 國票.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-10

### Page 1

請參閱末頁之聲明

2026.09.10 
洪有辰 C00270@ibfs.com.tw 
8103.TT 瀚荃 
 瀚海智聯，荃新超越

[財務資訊更新，座談會更新]   
   公司簡介 
瀚荃為全球知名的精密電子連接器、軟性排線(FFC)與線纜組件製造大廠，公司以自有
品牌 CviLux 行銷全球，近年成功從傳統的個人電腦零組件廠商，轉型為跨足 AI 伺服
器 、 低軌衛星 、 工業控制 、 車用電子等高附加價值領域的關鍵零組件解決方案供應商 。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 158 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作 ，138 
【大盤指數/股價】47183 / 113

➢ 2Q26 北美 CSP 廠拉貨動能強勁，高毛利產品占比急增 
2Q26 營收 10.97 億元，QoQ+20.6%，YoY+29%，其中佔比最高的伺
服器/網通與佔比次高的工業電子營收 YoY 分別為+82%與+19%；1Q26
貴金屬價格較佳，使 2Q26 的下腳料處分金額減少，加上正值新產品導
入初期，毛利率 37%，QoQ-1ppts，YoY-0.5ppts；儘管如此，整體費
用率下滑至 20.8%，帶動稅後 EPS 上升至 1.58 元，QoQ+36.2%，
YoY+267.4%。 
➢ 3Q26 成長動能來自伺服器/網通與工業領域 
展望 3Q26，瀚荃成長動能主要來自 AI 伺服器電源連接器及工業自動化
需求。受惠高電流端子技術、客製化提升及與台系電源廠協同設計，伺
服器/網通營收預估 YoY+58.6%；工業領域受智慧倉儲及歐美製造回流
帶動，營收YoY+34.5%，筆電與汽車則持穩。預估3Q26 營收 11.48 億
元 ， 伺服器/網通及工業占比 49%、18%， 產品組合優化帶動毛利率升至
38.2%，QoQ+1.2ppts，EPS 上升至 2.25 元，QoQ+42.6%，YoY+68.2%。 
➢ AI 電源成長動能強，800V HVDC 帶動板端連接器需求續升 
AI 伺服器功耗提升使傳統 AC 架構面臨轉換效率低及熱損耗高等瓶頸，
800V HVDC將逐步普及 ，PSU、BBU、CBU 等電力設備亦將拆分為 Power 
Sidecar，以釋放機櫃空間。瀚荃主導 DC 板端連接器，受惠次世代伺服
器電力設備連接器需求增加，預估 2026 年網通/伺服器營收 16.31 億
元，YoY+35%，占總營收 38%；整體營收 43.39 億元，YoY+29.3%，
毛利率 38%、營益率 16.6%，稀釋後 EPS 7.37 元，YoY+56%。 
➢ 預估瀚荃2027 年 EPS 11.5 元，首次給予買進評等，目標價158 元 
投研部預估瀚荃 2027 年營收 55.51 億元 ，YoY+27.9%； 毛利率40.4%，
YoY+2.5ppts； 營業利益率20.45%，YoY+3.9ppts； 稅後淨利率16.25%，
YoY+3ppts；稅後 EPS 11.5 元。評價方面，觀察過去 5 年 PER 區間大
部分在 10X~22X，目前評價位於區間下緣的 10.4X，考量瀚荃的板端電
源連接器在新一代 AI 伺服器電源架構中具備關鍵地位 ， 再加上旗下客戶
對工業自動化的需求強勁 ， 因此可望享有網通/伺服器與工業領域的雙成
長動能，故給予首次買進投資建議，目標價 158 元(2026F PER 14X)。

8103 瀚荃 加權指數

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

公司簡介 
瀚荃成立於 1990 年，總部位於新北市淡水區，為全球知名的精密電子連接器、軟性
排線(FFC)與線纜組件製造大廠，公司以自有品牌 CviLux 行銷全球，近年成功從傳統
的個人電腦零組件廠商，轉型為跨足 AI 伺服器、低軌衛星、工業控制、車用電子等高
附加價值領域的關鍵零組件解決方案供應商。在海外佈局方面，公司的廠區包含中國
東莞、蘇州、重慶等，以及在東南亞的越南與寮國等地，目前公司將原定於蘇州廠生
產的筆電產能，逐步移往重慶廠生產，並規劃旗下的泰國廠於 2026 年底進行投產。
瀚荃在 2026 的營收結構當中，預估伺服器/網通比重 38%、筆電 18%、工業 17%、
汽車電子 3%、其他 14%。 
圖 1：瀚荃產品營收比重與投研部預估

資料來源：瀚荃年報、國票投顧預估 
2Q26 北美 CSP 廠拉貨動能強勁，高毛利產品佔比急增 
2Q26 營收 10.97 億元，QoQ+20.6%，YoY+29%，其中佔比最高(達 48%)的伺服器
/網通與佔比次高(達 20%)的工業電子營收 YoY 分別為+82%與+19%；毛利率 37%，
QoQ-1ppts，YoY-0.5ppts，因 1Q26 貴金屬價格較佳，使 2Q26 的下腳料處分金額
相較 1Q26 減少約 15%，再加上正值新產品導入初期，毛利率出現短暫下滑所致；營
業利益率 16.2%，QoQ-2ppts，YoY-0.3ppts，下滑原因來自 1Q26 支付收購子公司
全宇昕(6651)相關顧問費以及認列庫藏股轉讓員工費用 ， 使營業費用增加 ； 儘管如此 ，
整體費用率下滑至 20.8%，QoQ-3ppts，YoY-0.2ppts，帶動稅後 EPS 上升至 1.58
元，QoQ+36.2%，YoY+267.4%。 
2H26 成長動能來自伺服器/網通與工業領域 
展望 3Q26， 瀚荃最大的成長動能仍將來自AI 伺服器電源連接器與工業自動化相關需
求。公司具備高電流應用的特殊端子結構技術與專利，針對 DC 端所設計的板端連接
器可望受惠於客製化增加與複雜度上升，並持續透過與多家台系電源企業協同設計而
間接切入輝達與北美 CSP 大廠供應鏈，因此投研部預估相關產品的 ASP 上升將使伺
服器/網通在 3Q26 的營收 YoY+58.6%；工業領域包含自動控制、風電與醫療等少量
多樣的產品，預計將持續受惠於智慧倉儲及歐美製造業回流帶動的自動化需求，相關
營收 YoY+34.5%。致於筆電與汽車業務的表現則相對平穩。投研部預估 3Q26 營收
為 11.48 億元，其中伺服器/網通佔比 49%，工業領域佔比為 18%；由於伺服器/網通
與工業領域的毛利率偏高，預估分別為 50%與 45%，在產品組合優化的情況下，投
研部預估 3Q26 毛利率為 38.2%，QoQ+1.2ppts，預估將帶動稅後 EPS 由 2Q26 的
1.58 元上升至 3Q26 的 2.25 元，QoQ+42.6%，YoY+68.2%。

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
AI 電源成長動能強，800V HVDC 帶動板端連接器需求持續上升 
圖 2：AI 伺服器 800V DC 電源架構

資料來源：瀚荃法說會簡報、Digitimes 
當前部分 AI 伺服器供電仍採用 AC 電源架構，但隨著伺服器功耗突破千瓦級，傳統
AC 配電系統開始浮現轉換效率偏低與熱耗損攀升的技術瓶頸，而 800V HVDC( 高壓
直流電源)未來將逐漸普及，電流在進入白空間的機櫃後直接以 DC 形式傳送，以減少
AC 與 DC 之間的轉換次數，同時 PSU(電源供應器)、BBU(電池備援單元)與 CBU(電
容備援單元)等電力設備會逐漸從 Compute Rack 拆分成獨立的 Power Sidecar ，並
放置於背板或與匯流排連接，以釋出更多伺服器空間放置更多數量的其他電子元件。
連接器在傳統的伺服器內多為標準品， AC 市電端的連接器與線材由國外大廠如 TE 
Connectivity(TEL.US)與 Amphenol(APH.US)主導，但 DC 板端則由台廠的瀚荃所主
導，尤其是在次世代技術中，單一機櫃內的 PSU、BBU 與 CBU 等所需的板端連接器
數量將明顯提升，因此投研部預估公司 2026 年的網通/伺服器營收為 16.31 億元，
YoY+35%，佔總營收 38%，2026 年預估營收 43.39 億元，YoY+29.3%；因產品組
合 優 化 且 中 國 華 南 地 區 廠 房 產 線 整 合 的 情 況 下 ， 預 估 毛 利 率 將 上 升 至38% ，
YoY+0.6ppts； 營業利益率16.6%，YoY+3.2ppts； 稅後淨利率13.3%，YoY+3.2ppts；
稀釋後 EPS 7.37 元，YoY+87.1%。 
圖 3：瀚荃產品營收比重與投研部預估

資料來源：瀚荃法說會簡報、Digitimes

預估瀚荃 2027 年 EPS 11.5 元，首次給予買進評等，目標價 158 元 
由於伺服器/網通與工業領域的營收在 2027 年預估 YoY+89.4%與 37%，將使此兩大
領域占比分別達 56%與 23%， 持續為公司成長的動力來源 ， 因此投研部預估瀚荃 2027
年營收 55.51 億元 ，YoY+27.9%； 毛利率40.4%，YoY+2.5ppts； 營業利益率20.45%，
YoY+3.9ppts；稅後淨利率 16.25%，YoY+3ppts；稅後 EPS 11.5 元，。評價方面，
觀察過去 5 年 PER 區間大部分在 10X~22X，目前評價位於區間下緣的 10.4X，考量
瀚荃的板端電源連接器在新一代 AI 伺服器電源架構中具備關鍵地位，再加上旗下客
戶對工業自動化的需求強勁 ， 因此可望享有網通/伺服器與工業領域的雙成長動能 ， 故
給予首次買進投資建議，目標價 158 元(2026F PER 14X)，YoY+56%。

個股與大盤走勢比較圖

資料來源：CMoney，玉山投顧

蘇怡禎  
syj214134-71478@esunsec.com.tw

研 究  報 告                
福懋科(8131.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
記憶體廠產能擴充，同步推升封測代工需求 
 投資評價與建議 
短期受惠記憶體供需緊張，中⻑期取決五廠產能開出與先進封裝量產進度，參
考相關封測同業PBR介於1~3倍，給予2.3倍PBR。 
 重點評論分析 
2Q26營收與獲利維持成⻑ ， 惟原料上漲限制毛利率擴張 ：2Q26營收季增7.2%
至31.57億元，年增50.2%，受惠為銷售量及平均代工價調漲；毛利率與上季持
平為19.6%，主因原材料持續走升抵銷代工漲價效益，稅後EPS 0.97元。7~8
月營收累計達成率69%，優於預期，隨著代工價格調漲陸續反映原材料成本，
推估3Q26/4Q26分別季增6.6%/8.3%至33.67/36.48億元，毛利率19.8% / 
20%，稅後EPS 1.06/ 1.17元。 
記憶體需求遠大於供給，為代工封測迎來正向循環：AI應用持續擴張帶動記憶
體市場需求⼤幅成⻑，AI伺服器及⼀般伺服器對HBM及RDIMM需求排擠⼀般
型消費性記憶體，在國內廠商積極提高產出與擴充產能下，未來將同步拉升封
測代工需求。此外，模組產品代工方面，因顆粒價格上漲，使終端消費性電子
需求放緩；僅伺服器與工控記憶體模組需求穩定。目前四座工廠 （⼀廠、⼆廠、
3A、3B）稼動率達90%以上，主要產品以DDR4為主、DDR3為輔，並持續淘
汰低附加價值產品、將增加DDR4相關設備並進行廠內擴充；新五廠則預計明
年初量產貢獻營收。 
 投資風險 
新增折舊與原材料成本上升壓抑獲利、記憶體景氣及價格反轉 
主要財務數據及預估值

| 營運概況  
2Q26 營收與獲利維持成，惟原料上漲限制毛利率擴張：2Q26 營收季增 7.2%至 31.57 億元，年增 50.2%，受惠
為銷售量及平均代工價調漲 ； 毛利率與上季持平為 19.6% ， 主因原材料持續走升抵銷代工漲價效益 ， 營業費用 1.35
億元，季增 33.7%，因銷管、研發費增加，營業利益率 15.4%，稅後淨利 4.29 億元，稅後 EPS 0.97 元。目前 7~8
月營收累計達成率 69%，優於預期，隨著代工價格調漲陸續反映原材料成本，推估 3Q26/4Q26 分別季增
6.6%/8.3%至 33.67/36.48 億元，毛利率 19.8% / 20%，稅後 EPS 1.06/ 1.17 元。 
記憶體需求遠大於供給，為代工封測迎來正向循環：由於 AI 應用持續擴張帶動記憶體市場需求⼤幅成⻑，AI 伺服
器及⼀般伺服器對 HBM 及 RDIMM 需求排擠⼀般型消費性記憶體產品供應，在國內廠商積極提高產出與擴充產
能下，未來將同步拉升封測代工需求。此外，模組產品代工市場方面，因顆粒價格上漲，使得終端消費性電子需
求放緩；僅伺服器與工控記憶體模組的訂單需求穩定。目前四座工廠 （⼀廠、⼆廠、3A、3B） 稼動率達 90%以上，
主要產品以 DDR4 為主、DDR3 為輔，並持續淘汰低附加價值產品、將增加 DDR4 相關設備並進行廠內擴充；新
五廠，則預計明年初量產貢獻營收。另傳統銲線封裝 Wire bond 產能持續擴充外，先進封裝製程設備於 3Q26 裝
機，4Q26 完成機台與產品驗證，預計今年小量生產。 
在製程技術方面，矽穿孔技術(3D TSV)與露銅製程(BVR)已裝機完成，4Q26 進入產品驗證，並完成送樣給客戶。
另覆晶封裝四層晶片堆疊方面，正做量產準備與送樣，預計 2Q27 進行驗證，開發產品應用於 DDR5 RDIMM 模
組產品。此外，為因應未來 DDR5 至 DDR 6 步入 Flip Chip 技術，公司提早佈局晶圓凸塊(Bump)與重佈線(RDL)
其製程產線(⼆廠無塵室)於第三季開始裝機與製程驗證，將拓展多樣化產品代工，開發晶片凸塊與高腳數封裝體之
特殊應用 ASIC， 由現有記憶體產品延伸至覆晶邏輯封裝代工領域。 
短期受惠記憶體供需緊張，中⻑期取決五廠產能開出與先進封裝進度：五廠已完成廠房建置，其中，⼆樓預計於
今年下半年完成裝機、驗證並於明年初開始量產，四、六、七樓則預計 2H27 陸續貢獻，故並非⼀次性全開出。
隨著代工價格陸續反映原料成本，且五廠高階產能將於明年初正式貢獻量產營收，若五廠全數開出後月營收有望
相較目前翻倍成⻑，但實際貢獻速度仍取決於客戶晶圓產能開出進度(產能開出預計明年中以後)。預估 2027 年營
收 173.5 億元，毛利率 20.54%，稅後 EPS 5.76 元，維持「增加持股」之投資評等，參考相關封測同業 PBR 介於
1~3 倍，目標價 70 元(2.3 倍 PBR*2027BVPS(F))。

9910 豐泰(FENG TAY)

報告日期： 2026/09/15 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.14 元 
豐泰 07~08/2026 營收金額 73.94 億元與 73.7 億元， YoY+1.00%
與-2.79%，MoM+8.12% 與-0.32%， 預估豐泰3Q26 營收金額 216.3
億元， YoY-0.23% ，QoQ+6.13% ，預估營業利益 16.18 億元，
YoY-24.29% ， QoQ+34.14% ，預估稅後盈餘 11.26 億元，
YoY-35.29% ，QoQ+11.02% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.14 元。

二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.15 元 
進入 4Q26 傳統淡季，預估豐泰 4Q26 營收金額 207.55 億元，
YoY+0.22% ， QoQ-4.05% ，預估營業利益 15.10 億元，
YoY-4.17% ， QoQ-6.71% ，預估稅後盈餘 11.34 億元，
YoY-20.56% ，QoQ+0.77% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.15 元。

三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.00 元 
豐泰 2026 年在主要客戶營運仍處於調整階段 ， 預期整體出貨量落
在 1.7 億雙，呈現小幅下滑，拖累豐泰 2026 年營運。預估豐泰
2026 年營收金額 820.00 億元， YoY-1.81% ，預估營業利益 51.03
億元， YoY-31.38% ，預估稅後盈餘 39.48 億元， YoY-21.61% ，預
估稅後 EPS 4.00 元。

四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 5.50 元 
豐泰 2027 年在主要客戶訂單恢復成長態勢 ， 加上其餘小型客戶訂
單持續成長，豐泰 2027 年營運將恢復成長。預估豐泰 2027 年營
收金額 880.00 億元， YoY+7.32% ，預估營業利益 69.73 億元，
YoY+36.64% ，預估稅後盈餘 54.28 億元， YoY+37.48% ，預估稅
後 EPS 5.50 元。

五. 投資建議區間操作，區間價 58~78 元 
觀察豐泰歷史 PER12~35X ，2026 年 ROE 16% ，PER16.9X ，
2027 年 ROE 19% ，PER 12.3X ，評價已達合理水準，故投資建
議區間操作，區間價 58~78 元。 (14.5~19.5X 2026 年稅後 EPS)

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
豐泰企業成立於  1971 年，資本額 98.75 億元，以製造運動鞋為
主要業務，總部設於雲林科技工業園區，從事產品開發及培育跨
國企業的管理人才；量產工廠遍佈中國、越南、印尼與印度。企
業員工人數近  130,000 名，生產之運動鞋數量占主要客戶 NIKE 
全球銷售量的六分之一強。 2006 年起跨足鞋類以外的製造領域，
開始經營高爾夫球、足球，冰上曲棍球用頭盔與器材等運動用品
的製造生產，逐步發展製造冰上曲棍球全系列產品，並具備高階
球之製造能力，包含 Nike 最高階足球 ─Nike Ordem 。豐泰企業自
2012 年起積極投入先進製程技術研究，逐年增加研發投資金額，
從早期的傳統製造發展至以技術領導生產的營運模式。研發機台
陸續投入量產測試，整體自動化技術已逐漸提昇。

二.產能概況

預估 2026 年生產量 1.17 億雙， YoY-2.5%

豐泰生產基地主要位於中國大陸、越南、印尼與印度。中國大陸
於 1988 年開始投資設廠，先後於福建省莆田市、福州市、江蘇
省太倉市設立福建荔豐鞋業開發有限公司、福建協豐鞋業有限公
司、福建三豐鞋業有限公司、蘇州裕豐塑膠科技有限公司、福建
萬豐鞋業有限公司、福建省莆田協豐模具有限公司。

越南共有七座工廠（ Dona Victor 、Dona Pacific 、Dona Orient 、
Dona Standard 、Dona Victor Molds 、Vung Tau Orient 、Shoe 
Majesty ），越南主要生產多功能運動鞋與籃球鞋為主，經營策略
提升生產效率和生產高難度鞋型的能力，建立自動化生產的基礎
能力。

印尼共有一座工廠（ P.T. Feng Tay ），主要生產兒童鞋為主，提升
生產與技術能力。印度方面，共有五座工廠（ CSD、Lotus 、East 
Wind 、Fair way 與 Tindvanam Footwear ），主要生產中低階運動
鞋與休閒鞋為主，穩定成長動能。

豐泰 2025 年生產量 1.2 億雙， YoY+2.6% 。預期 2026 年，在主
要客戶訂單仍呈現小幅衰退，其餘客戶出貨增加，交互抵銷，預
估豐泰 2026 年生產量 1.17 億雙，YoY-2.50% 。預期 2027 年訂單
恢復成長，全年生產量 1.22 億雙， YoY+4.3% 。

三. 2026 年營運概況與展望

預估鞋類製造 2026 年營收金額 768 億元， YoY-3.18%

豐泰運動鞋主要客戶為 NIKE 、休閒鞋與運動器材鞋類（非運動鞋
類）主要客戶為 Rockport 、 Tacnica 、 Bauer 、 Converse 與
Salomon 。其中 NIKE 占 88~90% ，非 NIKE 客戶占 10~12% 。豐
泰 2025 年鞋類營收金額 793.2 億元 ，YoY-4.86% 。 觀察豐泰2025
年出貨量 1.2 億雙 ，YoY+2.6% ， 在出貨單價下滑與台幣升值因素 ， 
導致單價下滑 7~8% 。

Nike 06/30/2026 公告 2026 年財報（ 06/2025~05/2026 ）：

1， Nike 4Q26 營收年減 1%至 109.7 億美元，優於分析師所估
108.6 億美元，淨利從去年同期 2.11 億美元增加到 10.7 億美
元，調整後  EPS 20 美分，優於分析師所估的  13 美分。全年淨
利為  31.1 億美元，略低於前一年度的  32.2 億美元。第  4 季毛
利率提高  8.9 個百分點，主要受惠於美國最高法院推翻川普政府
多項全球關稅措施後認列 9.86 億美元的預期退稅款 。 退稅使本季
每股盈餘增加  52 美分。不過分析師在預估調整後  EPS 時，已
排除此項一次性利益。

2，觀察 Nike 2026 年各地區營收概況，北美地區 205.11 億美元，
YoY+5% ；歐洲地區 125.72 億美元， YoY+3% ；中國地區 58.47
億美元 ，YoY-11%， 亞太與拉丁美洲地區62.43 億美元 ，YoY+0%。

3，觀察 NIKE 2026 年(06/2025~05/2026) 鞋類營收金額 295.25
億美元，呈現持平。觀察各地區鞋營收概況：北美地區鞋營收金
額 133.17 億美元， YoY+5.0% ；EMEA( 歐洲、中東及非洲 )鞋營收

YoY-13% ； APLA( 亞太與拉美 ) 鞋營收金額 43.77 億美元，

## Document 271: GFHK - Apple update

### Page 1

iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max Wait-Time Lukewarm

Maintained  Hold  
 Target price $369

iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max wait -time lukewarm: iPhone 18 Pro and iPhone 18 Pro Max pre -
orders began on Sept 12 in the U.S. and many other countries. Generally speaking, the wati-
time appears lukewarm, with: 1) 3–4 weeks for 256GB/512GB Pro Max across all colors, as 
well as Burgundy 1TB in the U.S.；2) 2–3 weeks for Glacier 256GB/512GB Pro Max in China; 
3) 1–2 weeks for Black/Glacier 1TB Pro Max in the U.S. ；4) immediate availability for Silver 
1TB and most 2TB Pro Max models in the U.S, as well as most iPhone 18 Pro configurations 
except for Burgundy 256GB in the U.S . We attribute the lukewarm wait -time to limited spec 
upgrade, higher pricing (especially for large storage models), etc. We also expect the Pro Max 
to be impacted by iPhone Duo and the criticism of heavy weight.  
iPhone Duo feedback generally positive, yet we lower the  build plan: The iPhone Duo, 
priced at $1,999 in the U.S., opened pre ‑orders on Oct 16 and officially launched on Oct 23. 
Early reviews were positive, highlighting its polished folding experience, high ‑quality inner 
display, and improved multitasking in iO S 27, but also point to trade ‑offs including a heavier 
and thicker form factor, a visible crease, and a dual‑camera. Due to hinge constraints, we are 
reducing the iPhone Duo EMS build plan from 7m to 6m units in 2026. We also lower the 
iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Ma x build plan to 72m units given the constraints associated with the 
adjustable‑aperture camera. 
Maintain Hold: Driven by investors’ appetite being away from AI capex themes, Apple share 
price outperformed Nasdaq by 12% since July. Fundamentally, market’s worry about memory 
cost is being eased but should persist, in our view. We estimate the DRAM/NAND cost to be 
up from $0.37 per Gb/$0.09 per GB in iPhone 17 (initial launch) to $1.5 per Gb/$0.25 per GB 
in iPhone 18 Pro, suggesting likely cost pressure on current pricing. Valuation wise, the share 
price trades at 32x FY27E P/E with 20% earnings CAGR, suggesting the stock isn ’t cheap. 
We maintain earnings estimates and TP of $369, based on 35x FY27E.  
Risks: 1) weak demand, 2) competition, 3) product delays, 4) geo-tension.

Jeff Pu, CFA 
SFC CE No. BNO719 
jeffpu@gfgroup.com.hk 
Henry Huang 
SFC CE No. BYA749 
henryhuang@gfgroup.com.hk
